## §1. Level-2 Layer Distinction calibration (S88 W8-88)

> **Parent rule**: `.claude/rules/cross-pillar-bridge-anatomy.md §"Three-Level Structural-Confidence Ladder" → §"Level-2 Layer Distinction"`. The rule statement (Level-2-binding vs Level-2-non-binding sub-class declaration MANDATORY at plan-freeze; bare-decomposition envelopes ineligible for registry-PASS) lives in the parent. The substitution chain + counter-example pattern + cross-link enforcement live here.

### Substitution chain (cohomology-class binding; substrate-physics derivation)

Per `math-scripts.md §"Double-Check Logic Before Compute"`:

- **Step 1 (definition)**: HKR : HH^*(A^{≤L}) → H^*_{dR}(continuum-image) is the Hochschild-Kostant-Rosenberg map of classical NCG (Connes-Moscovici 1995 §III.4 finite-spectral-triple residue formula context). It maps periodic Hochschild cohomology of the finite-L spectral algebra to de Rham cohomology of the continuum image.
- **Step 2 (definition)**: Level-1 of the cross-pillar bridge ladder states `[ε_substrate-IS] ↔ HKR-image[ε_laboratory-IN]` at the cohomology-class level (regulator-invariant, L-independent). The identity holds at every L_max.
- **Step 3 (substitution)**: A `L^{-α}` envelope on `‖HKR(c_L) − c_continuum‖` IS a Level-2-binding envelope iff `c_continuum` is the HKR-image of the Level-1 cohomology class. The envelope describes convergence of the Level-1 binding under the bridge map's `L → ∞` limit.
- **Step 4 (simplification)**: A `L^{-α}` envelope on `Tr(D_K^{-2s})` (substrate-internal Mellin moment, no HKR image to a continuum laboratory observable on the partner pillar) does NOT bind Level-1; it is a bare-decomposition envelope. The substrate-internal limit `c_∞ = lim_{L→∞} Tr(D_K^{<=L,-2s})` is an INTRINSIC substrate quantity, not a laboratory image.
- **Step 5 (direction)**: registry-PASS criterion `Level-3 < Level-2 envelope at canonical L_max` is MEANINGFUL ONLY for Level-2-binding envelopes; applying it to Level-2-non-binding envelopes admits false-PASS (the empirical Level-3 anchor passes a numerical bound on a quantity that does not bind to the laboratory measurement). Therefore the layer distinction MUST be enforced at the audit level.

### Calibration instances

The HKR `L_max → ∞` image binds the HP^1 cohomology class to the Peotta-Törmä quantum-metric trace; the empirical W5-6 atlas match 0.0095% F_4 strict at L_max=10 satisfies the binding envelope. Level-2-binding declaration MANDATORY (retroactively documented; pre-S88-W8-88 entry grandfathered with the sub-class flag).
- **Instance #2 (negative; counter-example pattern, no current registry entry)**: a hypothetical `L^{-α}` envelope on `Tr(D_K^{-2s})` evaluated at substrate-distance pole s=3 with no HKR image to a continuum lab observable on a partner pillar would be Level-2-non-binding and FAIL plan-freeze. Future cross-pillar bridge candidates (FWD-C1 / FWD-C2 / FWD-C3 per §4 below) must demonstrate Level-2-binding via explicit HKR / Connes-Karoubi pairing citation.

### Audit-script extension queue

`computations/_shared/_cross_pillar_bridge_audit.py` (S86 W-5 AUDIT-1 SCAFFOLD; existing post-W7a-73 with OE-form regex extension) is forward-extensible at S88 W8-88 to test the Level-2 sub-class declaration; the extension is queued as `S89-CROSS-PILLAR-BRIDGE-AUDIT-LEVEL2-SUB-CLASS-CHECK` and lands the regex-based detector for the `Level-2-binding` / `Level-2-non-binding` declaration tag in the bridge-anatomy block.

### Substrate framing (cross-link to phononic-framing.md §"IS Space, Not IN Space")

Level-2-binding vs Level-2-non-binding is a STRUCTURAL property of the bridge map at the substrate ↔ laboratory layer pair. The substrate-IS observable on Pillar A binds to the laboratory-IN observable on Pillar B ONLY via HKR-image bridge maps (or analogous Connes-Karoubi / K-theory boundary maps); bare-decomposition envelopes describe substrate-internal Mellin-truncation convergence without binding to any continuum laboratory. The direction of explanation flows:

```
Substrate (Pillar A) IS the [substrate-IS observable]
   → Bridge map (HKR; Level-2-binding REQUIRED)
   → Laboratory (Pillar B) IN [laboratory-IN observable]
```

A bare-decomposition envelope (Level-2-non-binding) lacks the bridge-map step; the would-be `c_continuum` reference is undefined, and the substrate-internal limit `c_∞` is NOT a laboratory observable. Treating such an envelope as registry-PASS-eligible inverts the substrate ↔ laboratory direction (a container-thinking violation per `phononic-framing.md`).

## §2. Element 2 OE-form discipline — K=3 calibration corpus (S88 W7a-73 baseline + S90 W1-13 advancement)

> **Parent rule**: `cross-pillar-bridge-anatomy.md §"IS-not-IN Anatomy (5 elements)" → §"Element 2 OE-form discipline"`. The rule statement (Element 2 must be OE-form: `\int.*d.*Tr.*\([ΠP]_<index>\)`; prose-only forbidden) lives in the parent. The K=3 worked-example calibration regex corpus + audit-script extension live here.

### Calibration corpus (K=3)

| # | Bridge | Element 2 form | Positive-match regex | Verdict |
|:-:|:-------|:---------------|:--------------------:|:-------:|
| 1 | W-5 §VII.W (Pillar III ↔ Pillar IV) | `R_geom(τ_fold) = ∫_BZ Tr g_ab^{(P_0)}(k; τ_fold) d^d k` | matches `\int.*d.*Tr.*\(P_0\)` | **PASS** (calibration baseline) |
| 2 | W11-5 FWD-C3 (Pillar IV ↔ Pillar V; pre-retrofit) | "Caroli-Matricon ladder asymmetry (W11-C5)" / "µSR chirality discrimination (W11-C6)" (prose-only) | does NOT match positive-match; matches negative-match | **FAIL** (calibration counter-example; requires §W7a-75 retrofit to projector form `Π^{vortex}_{B-phase}` + `Π^{µSR}_{A-phase}`) |
| 3 | S89 W7c emission #3 (§VII.AV rerouted; HIT K-counter K=2→K=3) | `∫_BZ d^d k Tr(P_n-s-substrate-distance-1) · ρ_BZ(k; τ_fold)` | matches `\int.*d.*Tr.*\([ΠP]_[a-z0-9_-]+\)` PASS | **PASS** (VALIDATED-W7c-EMISSION-3-LEXICAL; audit_sha256=`cc18126581ddd9a1ea0fa9f92e4d881219773fc363f749be082c8f2b429cc61d` at `s89_gate_verdicts.txt:143`) |

The 3-instance corpus saturates the K=3 MANDATORY-promotion threshold per `feedback_rules-compensate-missing-structure.md` (K_promotion=3). With the W7c emission #3 row landing, the binary-classifier calibration carries (i) a baseline PASS exemplar (W-5), (ii) a counter-example FAIL exemplar (W11-5 pre-retrofit), AND (iii) a substrate-distance-1 PASS instance with named projector `P_n-s-substrate-distance-1` and emergent-BZ integration domain. The Element 2 OE-form discipline remains MANDATORY at S88+ plan-freeze (the underlying status; K-counter advancement is structural-saturation of the calibration corpus, not status change).

### Audit pattern documentation (S90 W1-13 landing)

The W7c emission #3 lexical decomposes against the rule's positive-match regex per the substitution chain pre-registered at plan §W1-13 #10:

- **Step 1 — Definition**: Element 2 positive-match regex (rule canonical pattern) = `\int.*d.*Tr.*\([ΠP]_[a-z0-9_-]+\)`. Audit-script implementation (`_cross_pillar_bridge_audit.py:158`) expands to `(?:\\int|∫|\\sum|∑).*?(?:d.*?)?Tr.*?\([ΠP][_^].*?\)` accepting both LaTeX `\int` and Unicode `∫`.
- **Step 2 — Substitution**: W7c emission #3 string `∫_BZ d^d k Tr(P_n-s-substrate-distance-1) · ρ_BZ(k; τ_fold)`.
- **Step 3 — Simplify**: `\int` ↔ `∫` (integration-domain token) matches `∫`; `.*d.*` matches `_BZ d^d k `; `Tr.*\([ΠP]_[a-z0-9_-]+\)` matches `Tr(P_n-s-substrate-distance-1)` (subscript `n-s-substrate-distance-1` is dashes+letters+digits within `[a-z0-9_-]+`).
- **Step 4 — Direction**: regex match PASS → corpus row #3 VALIDATED-W7c-EMISSION-3-LEXICAL.

The substring `Tr(P_n-s-substrate-distance-1)` matches the positive regex per S88 W7a-73 K=2 MANDATORY discipline; the BZ-trace form `∫_BZ d^d k · ρ_BZ(k; τ_fold)` provides the integration-domain + named-projector structure mandated by Element-2 OE-form rule. The named projector `P_n-s-substrate-distance-1` is the substrate-IS image of the bridge map ι_* — the lab observable's structural form is pinned to the substrate sub-algebra at substrate-distance-1.

### Audit-script extension

`computations/_shared/_cross_pillar_bridge_audit.py` (S86 W-5 AUDIT-1 SCAFFOLD) is extended at S88 W7a-73 to include the OE-form regex pass on Element 2 text within each bridge §-anchor block:

- **Positive-match** REQUIRED: every Element 2 specification MUST contain at least one `\int.*d.*Tr.*\([ΠP]_<index>\)` operator-form match (or the `(\int|\sum).*` extension for degenerate Pillar V).
- **Negative-match** FORBIDDEN: any `Element 2.*: ...measurement|spectroscopy|test\.` prose-form sentence triggers FAIL on the bridge-entry audit.
- **Worked-example calibration**: K=2 corpus (W-5 PASS + W11-5 FAIL pre-retrofit) is used as audit-script test cases.

### Forward-looking convention pin

From S88 W7a-73 onward, all NEW cross-pillar bridge-theorem entries in `permanent-results-registry.md` MUST satisfy Element 2 OE-form regex at plan-freeze. Pre-S88 entries (W-5 §VII.W; W11-5 FWD-C3) are GRANDFATHERED with mandatory retrofit at §W7a-75 (W5-2 + W5-3 sidecars to projector form `Π^{vortex}_{B-phase}` and `Π^{µSR}_{A-phase}`). The OE-form discipline closes the W11-5 FWD-C3 REGISTRY-FAIL class of structural defect (Level-3 ratio_mismatch=1.029 violating Level-2 envelope 0.05 by ~21×) by construction at the rule-file level.

## §3. Hybrid Independence Test — K=1 calibration corpus (S88 W8-87)

> **Parent rule**: `cross-pillar-bridge-anatomy.md §"Forward template-adoption" → §"Hybrid Independence Test"`. The rule statement (calibration-corpus instance counts toward K-counter iff `(i ∨ ii ∨ iii) ∧ iv`: distinct substrate-IS pillar OR distinct laboratory-IN pillar OR distinct bridge map class, AND independent algebraic envelope) lives in the parent. The K=1 baseline corpus + retroactive §VII.AG.1 companion-tagging argument live here.

### Calibration corpus (K=1 at S88 W8-87)

| # | Registry entry | Substrate-IS pillar | Lab-IN pillar | Bridge map class | Algebraic envelope | (i) ∨ (ii) ∨ (iii) | (iv) | Independent? |
|:-:|:---------------|:--------------------|:--------------|:-----------------|:-------------------|:-------------------:|:----:|:------------:|
| 1 | §VII.AF.1 (S87 W5-1 / S86 W-5) | Pillar III (HP^1 cohomology on `(A_K^{≤L}, H_K^{≤L}, D_K^{≤L})`) | Pillar IV (Peotta-Törmä BZ-trace `R_geom`) | HKR `L_max → ∞` | `L^{-3}` at d=4 | (baseline) | (baseline) | **YES** (calibration #1) |
| Companion | §VII.AG.1 (S87 W6-1) | Pillar III (T7 cyclic-fold quotient on `(A_K^{≤L}, H_K^{≤L}, D_K^{≤L})`) | Pillar IV (S67 cyclic-fold image) | HKR `L_max → ∞` modulo cyclic-fold V_4 quotient | `L^{-3}` at d=4 (refinement of W-5 envelope under V_4 cyclic-fold quotient) | (i)=FAIL same Pillar III; (ii)=FAIL same Pillar IV; (iii)=FAIL same HKR class (V_4 quotient is REFINEMENT not new class) | (iv)=FAIL envelope is V_4-quotient REFINEMENT of W-5 `L^{-3}`, NOT independent | **NO** — tagged `SHARED-ANCHOR-COMPANION + PARTIAL-AXES-INSTANCE`; OUTSIDE K-counter |

### Substitution chain — §VII.AG.1 evaluation

Per `math-scripts.md §"Double-Check Logic"`; reproduces plan §W8-87 Steps 1–5.

- **Step 1** (Definition): K-counter advancement threshold = N=3 promotion to MANDATORY per `feedback_rules-compensate-missing-structure.md`.
- **Step 2** (Definition): "Distinct calibration instance" PRE-Hybrid-Independence-Test = each §VII registry entry citing the 5-IS-not-IN + 3-level discipline naively counted as one K-instance.
- **Step 3** (Substitution under `(i ∨ ii ∨ iii) ∧ iv`):
  - §VII.AG.1 substrate-IS pillar = Pillar III (T7 quotient on Jensen-deformed band-0 sector); §VII.AF.1 W-5 substrate-IS pillar = Pillar III (HP^1 cohomology on same sector). **MATCH ⇒ clause (i) FAILS.**
  - §VII.AG.1 laboratory-IN pillar = Pillar IV (S67 cyclic-fold image); §VII.AF.1 W-5 laboratory-IN pillar = Pillar IV (Peotta-Törmä BZ-trace). **MATCH ⇒ clause (ii) FAILS.**
  - §VII.AG.1 bridge map = HKR `L_max → ∞` modulo cyclic-fold V_4 (a quotient-functor REFINEMENT of W-5's HKR map); §VII.AF.1 W-5 bridge map = HKR `L_max → ∞`. The cyclic-fold V_4 quotient is a refinement of the same HKR class, not a structurally distinct bridge map class. **REFINEMENT-NOT-INDEPENDENT ⇒ clause (iii) FAILS.**
  - Disjunction `(i ∨ ii ∨ iii) = (FAIL ∨ FAIL ∨ FAIL) = FALSE`.
- **Step 4** (Simplify): Conjunction `FALSE ∧ iv = FALSE` regardless of clause (iv). §VII.AG.1 fails the Hybrid Independence Test.
- **Step 5** (Direction): K-counter does NOT advance for §VII.AG.1; therefore §VII.AG.1 is OUTSIDE the K-counter and gets the retroactive tag `SHARED-ANCHOR-COMPANION + PARTIAL-AXES-INSTANCE`. The K-counter advancement at S87 W6-1 close was naive narrative inflation that the Independence Test now formally excludes.

### Conclusion + forward enforcement

The retroactive tag `SHARED-ANCHOR-COMPANION + PARTIAL-AXES-INSTANCE` correctly classifies §VII.AG.1 outside the K-counter, preserving the structural integrity of the K-counter advancement (the K=3 corpus declared in §5 below remains W-5 + W11-5 + W4a-17 — all of which ARE independent under the Hybrid Independence Test). The Hybrid Independence Test enforces that K-counter promotion to MANDATORY tracks ONLY structurally-independent calibration instances; narrative inflation cannot drive premature MANDATORY-status.

- **Plan-freeze halt** on any S88+ K-counter advancement that does not document a per-clause Hybrid Independence Test verdict for the new instance.
- **Audit-script extension** queued for `computations/_shared/_cross_pillar_bridge_audit.py`: forward extension to verify that any S88+ K-counter row provides per-clause Independence Test pin (i/ii/iii/iv).
- **K-counter K=1 in this section** refers to the calibration-corpus state UNDER the Hybrid Independence Test as a stand-alone discipline (W-5 alone is the calibration baseline for the test itself); it does NOT supersede the K=3 MANDATORY corpus in §5 below, which was independently advanced via S88 W4a-17 close (2026-05-04). The two are not in conflict: the post-W4a-17 K=3 advancement IS consistent with the Hybrid Independence Test (W-5, W11-5, W4a-17 each satisfy `(i ∨ ii ∨ iii) ∧ iv`).

## §4. Three forward bridge candidates — FWD-C1/C2/C3 (S87 W5-5)

> **Parent rule**: `cross-pillar-bridge-anatomy.md §"Forward template-adoption" → §"Three forward bridge candidates for S88+ dispatch"`. The rule statement (each candidate pre-registers substrate-IS / laboratory-IN identifications + bridge map + algebraic envelope + empirical anchor target + inheritance-kernel rank declaration) lives in the parent. The detailed per-candidate pre-registration content lives here.

Each candidate is pre-registered with substrate-IS / laboratory-IN identifications, an explicit bridge map, an algebraic envelope (or structural-exact replacement), an empirical anchor target, and an inheritance-kernel rank declaration. > **Parent rule**: `cross-pillar-bridge-anatomy.md §"Algebra-axis orthogonality K-counter (parallel discipline; MANDATORY at K=3)"`. The N=3 calibration corpus + axiomatic-skeleton derivation + plan-freeze enforcement clauses live here.

### Status: MANDATORY at K=3

The algebra-axis orthogonality conjecture states: on any finite spectral triple `(A, H, D)` satisfying the 7 NCG axioms, the algebra-INVARIANT family (spectrum-only functionals `F({λ_k, m_k}) = Σ_k m_k g(λ_k)`; e.g., Seeley-DeWitt moments, ζ-residues, Mellin-Dirichlet identities) and the algebra-DEPENDENT family (state-pair functionals on `A`; e.g., Connes distance, state expectations, sample variances over occupation distributions) are STRUCTURALLY ORTHOGONAL in identity-class membership at the functional-class level — there is no closed-form `{λ_n}`-only identity reproducing any algebra-DEPENDENT functional, and conversely no state-functional-only identity reproducing any algebra-INVARIANT spectral moment.

At K=3 the corpus has saturated the structural pattern across three structurally-distinct calibration instances; the conjecture is hereby promoted to MANDATORY structural-theorem candidate (STAGE-1-CANDIDATE per `joint-theorem-promotion.md` 4-stage pathway, awaiting Stage-2 cross-axis independent-verify).

### Substrate-physics derivation (axiomatic skeleton)

NCG axioms 1+5 + Connes-Moscovici 1995 §III.4 dim-spectrum residue formula `a_n = Res[Tr(D^{−2s}); s = (d−n)/2] = Σ_k m_k λ_k^{−(d−n)}` GUARANTEE the algebra-INVARIANT family is non-trivial. NCG axioms 4+6 + Poincaré duality on `A_K` GUARANTEE the algebra-DEPENDENT family is non-trivial. The chirality-vs-A_F block-grading mismatch ensures `f(D²) ∩ π(A) = scalars` on the state-functional side, while the spectrum-only side is the full `Z(f(D²))` algebra. Both families are ALWAYS present; identity-class membership is structurally orthogonal by axiom-level NCG argument. (Full proof-mode rigorous derivation queued at S88 CF-D.)

Missing any of (1)-(4) → registry-incompleteness FAIL, plan-freeze halt with remediation request via `_corner_classification_audit.py` (S88 CF-E).

## §11 — Substrate-Input-Orthogonality Clause (Stage-2 PASS-AND ceiling)

**Provenance**: S88 W-23 W7c-167 V.1 (volovik-superfluid-universe-theorist; LEVEL-2 closure + obs1 PASS-AND independence workshop). Hardens the procedural-floor "without prior workshop context" guarantee (item 4 of `joint-theorem-promotion.md §"Two-Agent Independent-Verify"`) into a structural ceiling. Parent rule statement at `joint-theorem-promotion.md §"Substrate-input-orthogonality clause"`. Bidirectional cross-link to `pru-class-corpus.md §15` (full corpus); mirrored here for cross-pillar visibility because Stage-2 cross-axis verify is the joint-theorem analog of cross-pillar bridge auditing.

### Predicate

For any Stage-2 verification with N ≥ 2 observables {obs_1, ..., obs_N}, the procedural floor MUST be supplemented with the **substrate-input-orthogonality predicate**:

> ∃ obs_i such that the data file consumed by obs_i is loaded by exactly ONE cross-reviewer (NOT both).

PASS-AND across orthogonal-data observables is the structural ceiling for the procedural-floor independence guarantee. Without substrate-input orthogonality, Stage-2 PASS-AND establishes structural-OUTPUT-type independence (different decision pipelines on the same data) but NOT structural-INPUT independence (the data itself is shared). The calibration corpus advances under explicit "substrate-input-overlap caveat" tagging.

### Calibration corpus

#### Instance #1 — S88 W7c-167 obs1 PASS-AND with substrate-input-overlap caveat (Verdict B per W-23 §IV.3)

Stage-2 dispatch on §VII.AH STAGE-1-CANDIDATE (joint Path-(c) F_2-class theorem extended to multi-observable: obs1 IC-axis affine projection / obs2 anomaly s4-s2 integer-graded / obs3 Mellin residue ratio s3-s4). Cross-reviewers: mack-cosmic-bridge (spectral-side) + connes-ncg-theorist (axis-orthogonality side); both dispatched in parallel WITHOUT prior workshop context per `joint-theorem-promotion.md §"Two-Agent Independent-Verify"`.

| Observable | Data file | mack loads? | connes loads? | Substrate-input orthogonal? |
|:-----------|:----------|:-----------:|:-------------:|:---------------------------:|
| obs1 (IC-axis per-class affine projection) | `s87_w7_ic_per_class_verify.npz` SHA-256 `120435cbfd5ef313ebcba6e23ec1712c51d04a3f75b788034ebe7964aa32519f` | YES | YES | NO (shared) |
| obs2 (anomaly s4-s2 integer-graded factorized) | `s87_anomaly_s4_s2_data.npz` (ABSENT at dispatch-time) | — | — | PRE-REG-INC |
| obs3 (Mellin residue ratio s3-s4 pole-scope) | `s87_mellin_residue_s3_s4_data.npz` (ABSENT at dispatch-time) | — | — | PRE-REG-INC |

The substrate-input-orthogonality predicate FAILS at obs1 (both reviewers load the same `.npz` file + same registered §VII.AH text + same canonical_constants pins `xi_E_GGE_inv = 13.642473`, `tau_fold = 0.190`). PASS-AND on obs1 establishes structural-output-type independence (different decision pipelines on the same data: spectral-side Bayesian posterior vs axis-orthogonality-side parse-tree 4-corner classification) but NOT structural-input independence (the data itself is shared). Per W-23 §IV.3 Verdict B, the K=1 calibration corpus advances under explicit "substrate-input-overlap caveat" tagging.

### Forward enforcement

S89+ Stage-2 dispatches MUST verify substrate-input-orthogonality at plan-freeze:

1. Enumerate per-reviewer input-pin map; verify ≥ 1 observable's data file is in exactly one reviewer's map.
2. If all observables share data files, route to remediation: add an orthogonal-data observable (e.g., volovik consumes `3HeB-inheritance-canonical.md` cohomology-asymmetry data while connes consumes spectrum cache via Mellin pole interpretation NOT replicated by volovik) before Stage-2 dispatch.
3. Audit-script extension `_joint_theorem_independent_verify_audit.py` flags substrate-input-orthogonality predicate failures at plan-freeze with HARD-HALT remediation.

### Status

K=1 SUGGESTION (advisory) at S88 W7c-167. Promotes to MANDATORY at K=3 distinct calibration instances. Stage-2 PASS-AND verdicts emitted under SUGGESTION status carry an explicit **substrate-input-overlap caveat** when the predicate fails.

## §12 — Cross-Reviewer Audit-Machinery Self-Citation (6th audit item)

**Provenance**: S88 W-23 W7c-167 V.8 + B.60 (volovik-superfluid-universe-theorist; LEVEL-2 closure cross-reviewer-machinery audit-self-citation extension). Extends `joint-theorem-promotion.md §"Audit at plan-freeze"` from a 5-item list to a 6-item list. Parent rule statement at `joint-theorem-promotion.md §"Audit at plan-freeze"` item 6. Bidirectional cross-link to `pru-class-corpus.md §16` (full corpus); mirrored here for cross-pillar visibility per the §11 mirror precedent.

### Audit-machinery self-citation predicate

Cross-reviewer R applies "self-authored audit machinery" iff R applies a parse-tree decision procedure / 4-corner classification / cohomology bridge map at the verdict-emission layer AND R is the sole author of that machinery (e.g., the rule-file section defining it, the registry-entry that established it).

Self-authored audit machinery at the verdict-emission layer is structurally weaker than audit machinery cross-checked by another agent: the self-author has implicit interpretive authority over edge cases that an independent cross-checker would surface.

### Calibration corpus

#### Instance #1 — S88 W7c-167 connes-ncg axis-orthogonality side audit (per W-23 §IV.4 + §V.8)

Stage-2 dispatch on §VII.AH STAGE-1-CANDIDATE; connes-ncg axis-orthogonality side applies the §VII.U.2 4-corner parse-tree classification (connes-authored at S87 W-2 R3 close) at the verdict-emission layer. The application is admissible under joint-theorem-promotion item-3 (connes is NOT the original S86 W-9 workshop author for §VII.AH itself) but structurally weaker than a non-self-authored alternative. The §VII.U.2 classification machinery itself is connes-co-authored at S87 W-2 R3 close; connes applying it at the W7c-167 Stage-2 verdict-emission layer is structurally self-citation-at-machinery-layer.

| # | Source | Stage-2 dispatch | Self-citation observation | Calibration role |
|:-:|:-------|:-----------------|:---------------------------|:-----------------|
| 1 | S88 W7c-167 connes-ncg axis-orthogonality side audit (per W-23 §IV.4 + §V.8) | §VII.AH STAGE-1-CANDIDATE Stage-2 dispatch (mack spectral + connes axis-orthogonality) | connes-ncg applies `permanent-results-registry.md §VII.U.2` 4-corner parse-tree classification (connes-authored at S87 W-2 R3) at the verdict-emission layer; admissible under joint-theorem-promotion item-3 (connes is NOT the S86 W-9 workshop author for §VII.AH itself) but structurally weaker than a non-self-authored alternative | calibration corpus instance #1 of self-citation-at-machinery-layer pattern |
| 2 | reserved (S89+ §VII.AH re-dispatch with alternate machinery route or second-reviewer cross-check on §VII.U.2 application) | — | — | — |
| 3 | reserved | — | — | — |

### Forward enforcement

S89+ Stage-2 dispatches MUST verify the new 6th audit item at plan-freeze:

1. Identify per-reviewer audit machinery applied at the verdict-emission layer (parse-tree, 4-corner classification, cohomology bridge map, etc.).
2. Cross-reference each machinery's authorship trace via `mcp__knowledge__.trace_entity(<machinery-name>)` or registry/rule-file authorship section.
3. If ANY reviewer is the sole author of the machinery they apply at verdict-emission, route to remediation: (a) apply alternate machinery route at the verdict layer, OR (b) require a SECOND reviewer cross-checks the machinery application.
4. Audit-script extension `_joint_theorem_independent_verify_audit.py` flags self-citation predicate at plan-freeze.

### Status

K=1 SUGGESTION (advisory) at S88 W7c-167. Promotes to MANDATORY at K=3 distinct calibration instances per `feedback_rules-compensate-missing-structure.md`.

**Cross-link**: `joint-theorem-promotion.md §"Audit at plan-freeze"` items (1)-(6) (rule statement, item 6 added at W-23 V.8); W7c-167 working-paper §"Per-observable axis-orthogonality cross-review" + `axis-orthogonality-side verdict (connes-ncg-theorist)` block; atlas-12 §V.

| # | Observable name | Surface reading | Parse-tree closed form | Structural corner | Source |
|:-:|:----------------|:----------------|:------------------------|:------------------|:-------|
| 1 | `Var_a(n_a^GGE)` | state-history "GGE" → algebra-DEPENDENT (naïve parse) | `Σ_a (|v_a|² − ⟨|v_a|²⟩)²` = `Σ_a (Δ_BCS²/(2(λ_a²+Δ_BCS²)) − ⟨…⟩)²` (Bogoliubov closed form per S52 BdG canonical amplitudes) | **Corner II** (algebra-INVARIANT, s=4) | S89 W-3 + W-17 §V.2/V.3 reclassification |

The naïve-parser failure mode is **state-history-label-driven corner mis-classification**; the parse-tree audit forecloses it by construction.

### Enforcement provenance

Audit-script hook at `computations/_shared/_registry_landing_audit.py` (extended in S90 W1-8) regex-detects state-history label patterns in new §VII entry text; if a pattern matches AND a parse-tree expansion block (regex `Parse-tree expansion:|parse_tree_expansion:|## Parse-tree`) is ABSENT, fires `MISSING-PARSE-TREE-EXPANSION` diagnostic at S2 advisory severity, halting plan-freeze.

The audit script's extended pattern set adds: `\bBogoliubov-(state|amplitude|coefficient)\b`, `Δ_M\b` + `\bDelta_M\b` (ASCII variant), `α_s_route_[0-9]+` (regex generalization over per-route enumeration). ## §14. FOUR-rule cross-composition meta-pattern (S91 W1 CF-S91-W1-NEW-RULE landing)

> **Parent rule**: `.claude/rules/cross-pillar-bridge-anatomy.md §"Deferred-pending intermediate verdict-class" §"FOUR-rule cross-composition meta-pattern"`. The rule statement (admissibility predicate + K-counter advancement clause + Hybrid Independence Test reference) lives in the parent. The per-instance calibration corpus + K-counter status detail live here.

> **Provenance**: S91 W1 workshop `sessions/archive/session-91/workshops/s91-w1-operational-alignment-regulator-class-robustness.md` §Q-FINAL.b answer (lines 1215-1242; literal proposed rule-extension text at lines 1230-1238) + Workshop Verdict row 8 (line 1255) + §"What Changed (b) Structural changes" first bullet (line 1292) + CF spec CF-S91-W1-NEW-RULE at lines 1330-1334. Landed at S91 close (fix-in-session corrective discharge 2026-05-22) per `feedback_fix-in-session-never-defer.md`.

### FOUR-rule cross-composition admissibility predicate

A §VII entry satisfies the meta-pattern iff ALL FOUR of the following hold simultaneously on STRUCTURALLY ORTHOGONAL axes per algebra-axis orthogonality K=3 MANDATORY:

1. **Layer-separability carve-out** (`mechanical-closure-discipline.md §"Layer-separability carve-out"`): substrate-IS observable admits Type-F (single-summand-projection trace; algebra-INVARIANT) ↔ Type-S (state-pair functional; algebra-DEPENDENT) separation under layer-functor `F : substrate → methodology → audit`; Type-F sub-observable admits closed-form mechanical evaluation; Type-S requires numerical evaluation; L1-L4 admissibility conditions pre-registered.
2. **PROXY-REFINEMENT deferred-pending** (parent rule's §"Deferred-pending intermediate verdict-class" PROXY-REFINEMENT sub-class): Level-2 envelope currently realized via SCHEMATIC proxy OR Casimir-bound argument, PENDING refinement by FULL physical pipeline.
3. **OPERATIONAL-ALIGNMENT deferred-pending** (parent rule's §"Deferred-pending intermediate verdict-class" OPERATIONAL-ALIGNMENT sub-class): Level-2 envelope realized via operational-machinery state-side specification, PENDING refinement by K_canonical pin uniqueness operational-alignment.
4. **Three-Layer Regulator §VII.M L3-OBSERVABLE stratum inhabitation** (`permanent-results-registry.md §VII.M`; lizzi PRIMARY S83 PERMANENT-not-K-counter): substrate-IS observable INHABITS L3-OBSERVABLE stratum (algebra-INVARIANT spectrum-only-functional layer); INHABITATION not advancement (PERMANENT theorem, not SUGGESTION-class rule).

The cross-composition is structurally distinct from each constituent rule: a §VII entry inhabiting only ONE has weaker structural commitment than one inhabiting all FOUR. The four constituent rules' K-counters advance INDEPENDENTLY per their own per-instance corpora — they do NOT advance jointly via the meta-pattern's instances (per W1 Q-FINAL.b lines 1219-1226). The meta-pattern warrants its OWN K-counter, structurally orthogonal to the four constituent K-counters.

### Hybrid Independence Test application

K-counter advancement for this meta-pattern applies the Hybrid Independence Test `(i ∨ ii ∨ iii) ∧ iv` per §3 above: each new calibration instance MUST satisfy (i) distinct substrate-IS pillar from prior K-instances OR (ii) distinct laboratory-IN pillar OR (iii) distinct bridge map class, AND (iv) independent algebraic envelope. The disjunction captures structural diversity along substrate-axis / lab-axis / bridge-axis dimensions; the conjunction with (iv) enforces algebraic-envelope independence (purely numerical refinements do NOT advance K).

### K-counter status

**SUGGESTION at K=1** (S91 W1 close, 2026-05-22). Promotes to MANDATORY at K=3 distinct calibration instances per `feedback_rules-compensate-missing-structure.md` K-counter promotion threshold. Until promotion, the rule applies as authoring-time guidance for new §VII registry entries simultaneously inhabiting four constituent rule structures; audit-script extension at `_cross_pillar_bridge_audit.py` (queued S92+) fires at S2 advisory severity on detected cross-composition without explicit FOUR-rule pre-registration.

## §15. Level-3 anchor singleness sub-clause (S91 W4 CF-S92-W5-1-F landing)

> **Parent rule**: `.claude/rules/cross-pillar-bridge-anatomy.md §"Three-Level Structural-Confidence Ladder" §"Level 3 — Empirical Anchor at Canonical L_max" §"Level-3 anchor singleness sub-clause"`. The rule statement (Hybrid registry-text framing requires Level-3 anchor single-pinned at substrate-natural canonical source; Level-2-B sub-row table DIAGNOSTIC ONLY) lives in the parent. The per-instance calibration corpus + K-counter advancement + cross-references live here.

> **Provenance**: S91 W4 workshop `sessions/archive/session-91/workshops/s91-w4-w5-1-fail-falsification-vs-layer-orthogonality.md` §Q-VOL-1 answer lines 1117-1148 + Q-VOL-1 question lines 936-937 + §DISSENT #1 + Workshop Verdict table row 12 (line 1241) + CF spec CF-S92-W5-1-F at lines 1262 + 1334-1338. Landed at S91 close (fix-in-session corrective discharge 2026-05-22). Cross-link to `s88-pending-edits-ledger.md` "preserve K-window log-derivative anchor `-7.046336` as SOLE Corner-IV calibration source".

### Rule

For any §VII registry entry adopting the Hybrid (single-slot with regulator-class-keyed Level-2-B sub-row table) registry-text framing, the Level-3 empirical anchor MUST be single-pinned at the substrate-natural canonical source. The Level-2-B sub-row table is DIAGNOSTIC ONLY at the methodology-floor F-image axis; sub-row values MUST NOT be cross-referenced as Level-3 anchor co-primaries. Cross-corner co-primary at the Level-3 anchor axis is FORBIDDEN by the same substrate-first canonical sourcing direction (`substrate-first-canonical-sourcing.md §(i)`).

### Structural reading

The §"Algebra-axis orthogonality K-counter" MANDATORY at K=3 pins the substrate-IS observable identity at the `(algebra, projector, pole)` triple layer. The Hybrid (option iii) landing pattern extends this to a Level-3 anchor singleness discipline at the EMPIRICAL-ANCHOR axis. The two are STRUCTURALLY DIFFERENT layers (substrate-IS observable identity vs Level-3 empirical anchor) but the discipline is the SAME structural axiom: ONE canonical source per axis. Future Hybrid landings at Cell IV (and structurally analogous cells where Level-3 anchor singleness is licensed) MUST maintain this separation.

### Cross-link to Single-observable-per-triple structural filter

The two sub-rules operate at non-overlapping but reinforcing layers: the Single-observable-per-triple filter (corpus.md §16) operates at the SUBSTRATE-IS observable identity axis (forbidding slot-splits without discontinuity evidence); this sub-clause operates at the LEVEL-3 EMPIRICAL ANCHOR axis (forbidding Level-2-B regulator-class sub-row values from being cross-referenced as Level-3 co-primaries). Both rules collectively enforce ONE canonical source per axis at the empirical-test layer.

### K-counter status

**SUGGESTION at K=1** (S91 W4 close, 2026-05-22). The §VII.AV Hybrid landing is the canonical first calibration corpus instance. Promotes to MANDATORY at K=3 distinct calibration instances per `feedback_rules-compensate-missing-structure.md` K-counter promotion threshold. Forward-promotion gating on Hybrid landings at §VII.AU.OP-PROJ + §VII.AY.OP-PROJ + §VII.BA + §VII.AF.1.OP-PROJ (forward-queued candidates). Until promotion, the rule applies as authoring-time guidance for Hybrid registry-text landings at Cell IV; the audit-script extension at `_registry_landing_audit.py` (queued S92+) fires at S2 advisory severity on detected Hybrid landings without explicit Level-3 anchor singleness declaration.

## §16. Single-observable-per-triple structural filter (S91 W4 CF-S92-W5-1-C-EXT landing)

> **Parent rule**: `.claude/rules/cross-pillar-bridge-anatomy.md §"Single-observable-per-triple structural filter"`. The rule statement (continuous-deformation discriminator predicate; CONTINUOUS scan ⇒ slot-split FORBIDDEN; DISCONTINUOUS jump ⇒ slot-split LICENSED with explicit jump-location + structural-reason tag) lives in the parent. The per-instance calibration corpus + K-counter status + cross-references live here.

> **Provenance**: S91 W4 workshop `sessions/archive/session-91/workshops/s91-w4-w5-1-fail-falsification-vs-layer-orthogonality.md` §EMERGENCE (1) Connes sub-bullet (lines 1018-1059; literal pre-composed rule-extension structure) + V3 + Re:V3 continuous-deformation evidence (line 361-389; WP line 150 PV_mass → 0 kernel-verification at 4.7e-7 bit precision) + Re:V5 retraction of Reading B-strong (line 469) + Re:V4 clause-4 same-corner co-primary forbiddance (line 564-573) + Workshop Verdict table row 11/12 (line 1240-1241) + CF spec CF-S92-W5-1-C-EXT at lines 1260 + 1328-1332. Landed at S91 close (fix-in-session corrective discharge 2026-05-22).

### Structural reading

The §"Algebra-axis orthogonality K-counter" MANDATORY at K=3 (S87 W-2 R3 close) pins substrate-IS observable identity at the cohomology-class / parse-tree-structure layer (algebra-INVARIANT vs algebra-DEPENDENT 4-corner partition). It operates at the STRUCTURAL-AXIOM layer. This sub-section extends the same structural axiom to the EMPIRICAL-TEST layer for SLOT-SPLIT proposals — when a registry author proposes that observable values O₁ and O₂ at the same nominal `(algebra, projector, pole)` triple inhabit STRUCTURALLY DISTINCT substrate-IS observables (a "Reading B-strong" style slot-split into two §VII slots).

### Discriminator predicate

Given two candidate observable values O₁, O₂ at the SAME nominal `(algebra, projector, pole)` triple obtained under two distinct regulator-class evaluations R₁, R₂:

- IF a parameter scan over a deformation parameter p (regulator-mass, coupling, etc.) such that p → p₁ recovers O₁ and p → p₂ recovers O₂ produces a **CONTINUOUS** interpolation `O(p)` across all intermediate values (no discrete identity jump at any finite p), THEN the substrate-IS observable identity is SINGLE-PINNED at the triple; O₁ and O₂ are methodology-floor F-image variants of the SAME substrate-IS observable under the regulator-class axis. The §VII slot stays SINGLE; alternative readings land as Level-2-B DIAGNOSTIC sub-rows.
- IF the parameter scan produces a **DISCONTINUOUS** identity jump at some finite p (e.g., different parse-tree structure activates above/below a threshold), THEN the slot-split MAY be licensed; O₁ and O₂ inhabit structurally distinct substrate-IS observables. The slot split MUST then be tagged with explicit jump location + structural reason in the §VII entry's anatomy element 1.

### Cross-link to algebra-axis orthogonality

This rule extends the parent rule's §"Algebra-axis orthogonality K-counter" from the structural-axiom layer (4-corner partition; substrate-IS observable identity at the parse-tree layer) to the empirical-test layer (CONTINUOUS parameter scan as the licensing evidence for slot-split proposals). Both layers enforce the same structural commitment: ONE substrate-IS observable per `(algebra, projector, pole)` triple.

### Cross-link to Operator-Projection Reading-A Naming Hygiene

This rule complements `registry-landing.md §"Operator-Projection Reading-A Naming Hygiene"` MANDATORY K=3 with regulator-class-axis specialization. The Operator-Projection hygiene rule operates on the operator-side ↔ state-side distinction (algebra-INVARIANT vs algebra-DEPENDENT); this rule operates on the regulator-class-axis WITHIN the operator-side family (or within the state-side family). Both rules forbid silent slot-splits at their respective discriminator axes.

### K-counter status

**SUGGESTION at K=2** (advanced S96 W-1 close, 2026-05-30; was K=1 at S91 W4 close, 2026-05-22). The S91 W4 workshop verdict is the canonical first calibration corpus instance (continuous-deformation evidence at WP line 150 demonstrating PV_mass = 0 → M_KK → √2·M_KK continuous interpolation without discrete identity jump; §VII.AV slot remains SINGLE under K=3 MANDATORY algebra-axis orthogonality axiom; Reading B-strong slot-split proposal RETRACTED in full by Re:V3 + Re:V5 + Re:V4 clause-4). The S96 W-1 q_Ω workshop verdict is the second instance (instance #2 below): the cosmological deceleration readout q_Ω where the three routes' native q_Ω values are DIAGNOSTIC sub-rows of one partition-keyed observable, the route-variable (partition `x = ρ_s/ρ_n`) interpolates CONTINUOUSLY (no discrete identity jump), and the slot stays SINGLE. Promotes to MANDATORY at K=3 distinct calibration instances per `feedback_rules-compensate-missing-structure.md` K-counter promotion threshold. Until promotion, the rule applies as authoring-time guidance; the audit-script extension at `_cross_pillar_bridge_audit.py` (queued S92+) fires at S2 advisory severity on detected slot-split proposals without explicit parameter-scan + discontinuity-witness pre-registration.

### Calibration corpus instance #1 (canonical first instance)

**S91 W4 workshop verdict on §VII.AV at `(M_2(ℂ), P_BdG, s=4)` triple** (Pillar V BdG sub-algebra; substrate-distance-2 pole). W5-1 PV_mass scan from PV_mass = 0 (kernel-verification anchor returning `-7.046336 ± 4.7e-7` to bit precision) to PV_mass = (M_KK, √2·M_KK) (FULL-PV pipeline returning `-527.97` M_KK²) is CONTINUOUS; no discrete identity jump at any finite PV_mass per WP line 150 numerical-kernel-independence test (4.7e-7 deviation from `-7.046336` at PV_mass → 0 limit demonstrates bit-precision kernel verification). Reading B-strong slot-split proposal (Re:V4 § C2 original; Reading B-strong line 564-573) was RETRACTED in full per the continuous-deformation evidence (Re:V3 line 369; Re:V5 line 469). The §VII.AV slot remains SINGLE per K=3 MANDATORY algebra-axis orthogonality axiom; the divergence between B(R_SCHEMATIC) = `-7.046336` M_KK² and B(R_FULL-PV) = `-527.97` M_KK² IS a methodology-floor F-image divergence at the regulator-class axis per K=4 MANDATORY level-pin discipline.

### Calibration corpus instance #2 (S96 W-1 q_Ω deceleration readout)

> **Provenance**: S96 W-1 workshop `sessions/archive/session-96/workshops/w1-q-omega-band-divergence.md` (transit-dynamics-theorist + volovik-superfluid-universe-theorist, 2 rounds). Verdict Topic 2 = Converged-on-Reading-C; Re:T2 (volovik partition-mechanism), A3 (transit FUNCTIONAL-DEPENDENT confirmation), R2-B CONVERGENCE-3 + EMERGENCE E3 (volovik). Landed at S96 W-1 close (2026-05-30).

**S96 W-1 workshop verdict on the cosmological deceleration readout q_Ω** (the emergent-FRW back-reaction observable; not a Pillar-V BdG-pole triple — the substrate-IS "triple" here is the partition-keyed deceleration `q_Ω = ½(1−2x)/(1+x)` at the substrate texture, with the route formalism playing the role of the regulator-class axis). Three independent routes supply native q_Ω readouts: route-1 AOFT (declines q / partition traced out), route-2 Volovik two-fluid (`[−1.0, +0.50]`), route-3 GFT condensate (`[−0.2146, +1.2397]`). The candidate slot-split proposal would treat these three native q_Ω as THREE structurally distinct substrate-IS observables (three §VII entries). The discriminator predicate FORBIDS the split:

- **The route-variable is the superfluid/normal partition `x = ρ_s/ρ_n`** (Re:T2): q_Ω is partition-keyed, and each route models the partition differently. As `x` is scanned continuously from `x → 0` (vacuum-removed, `q → +½`) through `x_fold = 85.7928` (`q = −0.98272`) to `x → ∞` (vacuum-dominated, `q → −1`), `q_Ω(x)` interpolates CONTINUOUSLY (`dq_Ω/dx = −3/[2(1+x)²] < 0` strictly, no discrete identity jump at any finite x; Sage-exact, W1-4 + workshop R1). So the three routes' native q_Ω are NOT three distinct observables — they are ONE partition-keyed observable read at three (route-determined) partitions. The §VII slot stays SINGLE; the three native ranges are DIAGNOSTIC sub-rows.
- **The invariant is H²*, NOT q_Ω**: the first-moment H²* triangulates ≤0.04% across the three routes (partition-BLIND, depends on `ρ_tot = ρ_s + ρ_n`, the route-invariant Bogoliubov sum `ρ_relic = 26.5539`); q_Ω is the second-moment partition-KEYED readout. The route-invariance of H²* and route-dependence of q_Ω are the "sum vs ratio" of the same `(ρ_s, ρ_n)` — a SINGLE observable family, not a slot-split.
- **F-image divergence at the route-formalism axis**: the divergence between the native q_Ω ranges (+0.50 / +1.24 / OUT) is a methodology-floor F-image divergence at the route/partition axis (the cosmological analog of the regulator-class axis in instance #1) — NOT a substrate-IS observable-identity jump. This is the cosmological twin of the FUNCTIONAL-DEPENDENT S96-SDW-EFT-CONTROL pattern (same D_K spectrum → Gaussian FAIL / Mellin PASS / a-ratio-driver INFO; `audit 7d62a904…` superseded by `74158937…`).

**Cross-link to multiplicative-normalization cancellation** (`math-scripts.md §"Multiplicative-normalization cancellation invariants"`, MANDATORY K=3): q_Ω is the NON-factorizing case. It FAILS the `O = w(param)·g(K)` factorization premise because `ρ_s` and `ρ_n` are two INDEPENDENT thermodynamic functions of state (in two-fluid hydrodynamics `ρ_tot` and the superfluid fraction `ρ_s/ρ` are independent — `ρ_s/ρ` cannot be derived from `ρ_tot` alone; it needs the order-parameter texture). There is no common multiplicative factor to cancel ⇒ q_Ω is FUNCTIONAL-DEPENDENT (partition-keyed), the complement to the FUNCTIONAL-INVARIANT a-ratio driver (where `w(L_max)` cancels). This pins the W6-18 analogy precisely: q_Ω does NOT enjoy the cancellation because its two arguments are independent, not a pre-factor times a shared kernel.

Reserved K=3 row for a forward calibration instance at a third §VII entry where a slot-split proposal arises.

## §17. Within-cell discriminator axes (α/β/γ/δ) (S91 W2 CF-S92-WITHIN-CELL-DISCRIMINATOR-AXES-K1-SUGGESTION landing)

> **Parent rule**: `.claude/rules/cross-pillar-bridge-anatomy.md §"Within-cell discriminator axes (α/β/γ/δ)"`. The rule statement (four pairwise-independent within-cell discriminator axes; K=1 SUGGESTION status) lives in the parent. The per-axis substrate-physics definitions + axis-composition under layer-decomposition + W2 calibration corpus + cross-references live here.

> **Provenance**: S91 W2 workshop `sessions/archive/session-91/workshops/s91-w2-chi-prime-weight-canonical-substrate-derivation.md` §V4 lines 360-456 (axes α/β/γ; volovik PRIMARY) + §Re:V4 lines 838-868 (axis δ; van-den-dungen PRIMARY) + §R2 E-2 lines 1547-1561 (four-axis enumeration; volovik) + §R2-B E-vdd-2 lines 1981-2003 (van-den-dungen concur) + CF spec §"Carry-Forward Computations" #5 lines 2129-2134. Landed at S91 close (fix-in-session corrective discharge 2026-05-22).

### Structural reading

The parent §"Algebra-axis orthogonality K-counter" (MANDATORY at K=3 per S87 W-2 R3 close) operates at the **cross-cell** axis (Cell I/II/III/IV partition per `permanent-results-registry.md §VII.U.2` clause (e)). It is SILENT on within-cell discrimination — adjudication among multiple substrate-IS observables that all parse to the SAME corner cell. The S91 W2 χ'_weight workshop was the FIRST framework instance where within-cell canonical-weight discrimination was needed (all four candidate substrate-derivations 3/6, 5/14, 0.404, 5/8 parse to Cell I × s=3 by the parse-tree decision procedure — the K=3 MANDATORY clause does NOT supply a discriminator among them).

The workshop surfaced FOUR orthogonal substrate-priority axes that DO discriminate at the within-cell level. These axes operate within Cell I in the W2 instance; the framework expects them to apply uniformly to within-cell adjudication in any of the four cells.

### Axis (α) — K-theoretic vs representation-theoretic (V4 line 445)

Substrate-physics definition: Wedderburn-RANK invariants are preserved by Kasparov-KK projections (the morphism class to which inheritance morphisms χ' belong per `3HeB-inheritance-canonical.md` Step 3); HS-DIM invariants are NOT preserved by Kasparov-KK projections (different faithful representations give different HS-DIMs). The axis is LAYER-DEPENDENT (Re:V4 line 830-838): K-theoretic invariants are canonical at the K_0 functor layer; representation-theoretic invariants are canonical at the regular-representation trace functor layer (`Tr` on H_K is a substrate-IS canonical functional independent of K_0; it preserves trace-mass under the canonical χ'-pullback by construction). Canonical exemplar (W2 instance): Reading 1 (Wedderburn-RANK 3/6 = 0.500) is canonical at the K_0 layer; Reading 2 (HS-DIM 5/14 = 0.357143) is canonical at the regular-representation Tr layer.

### Axis (β) — source-side vs target-side (V4 line 447)

Substrate-physics definition: inheritance-restriction weight is structurally a SOURCE-SIDE mass-fraction surviving (how much of the source algebra's K-class-mass / trace-mass passes through the morphism), NOT a TARGET-SIDE representational-room ratio. The axis discriminates substrate-IS observables that share parse-tree structure but differ in which side of the morphism they evaluate. Canonical exemplar (W2 instance): Reading 1 (source-side K-rank 3/6) and Reading 2 (source-side trace-dim 5/14) are source-side; the hypothetical target-side K-rank ratio 3/4 = 0.75 (V3-rejected) and the D2 candidate target-side Hilbert-image-dim ratio 5/8 = 0.625 are target-side. Axis (β) DOWNGRADES target-side candidates relative to source-side ones for the inheritance-restriction-weight role.

### Axis (γ) — primary corridor (b) vs auxiliary corridor (c) (V4 line 449)

Substrate-physics definition: element-1 (b) χ'-pullback is the PRIMARY substrate-algebra-deformation corridor (e.g., CF-37 / T1.9 at (d)∘(b) for χ'_weight); element-1 (c) γ(s) ≠ Γ(s) modified-universal-kernel is the SECONDARY AUXILIARY corridor (e.g., T1.8 at (c)∘(d)). The axis discriminates the PRIMARY canonical from AUXILIARY canonicals at their own pre-registered deformation columns. Within the (b)-corridor PRIMARY, axis (γ) does NOT discriminate; it ONLY discriminates between the (b)-corridor and (c)-corridor as a whole. Canonical exemplar (W2 instance): Reading 1 (b-corridor Wedderburn-RANK) and Reading 2 (b-corridor HS-DIM) inhabit the PRIMARY (b)-corridor; Reading 3 (digamma-modulated 0.404) is the AUXILIARY (c)-corridor canonical at its own pre-registered deformation column.

### Axis (δ) — evaluator-trace-layer vs K_0-rank-layer (Re:V4 lines 854-862)

vdd's new contribution to the four-axis structure. Substrate-physics definition: this axis breaks the tie between axes (α) K_0 layer and (α) regular-representation Tr layer when both layers have admissible canonicals. The χ'_weight factor IN α'(M_LRD) lives at the regular-representation trace evaluator layer per the CM-1995 §III.4 residue formula's prescribed evaluator (the substrate H_K is framework-pinned as the canonical regular representation); at this evaluation layer, axis (δ) points to the trace-layer canonical (Reading 2's 5/14) rather than the K_0-layer canonical (Reading 1's 3/6). Axis (δ) is the structural realization of `epistemic-discipline.md §"Layer-Decomposition"` Phi correspondence at the substrate-physics-observable layer. Canonical exemplar (W2 instance): for the χ'_weight factor in α'(M_LRD)'s multiplicative decomposition, axis (δ) routes the within-Cell-I adjudication to Reading 2 (5/14) at the layer-where-α'(M_LRD)-lives, while Reading 1 (3/6) retains canonical status at the K_0 layer for the K-class-rank index of χ' (a structurally different question).

### Axis composition under layer-decomposition

The four axes are PAIRWISE INDEPENDENT at the methodology-rule layer per `epistemic-discipline.md §"Layer-Decomposition"` Phi-correspondence orthogonality. Composing (α) + (β) + (γ) + (δ) on the W2 canonical instance gives: axis (α) is layer-DEPENDENT (K_0 ↔ trace); axis (β) downgrades target-side candidates; axis (γ) separates (b) PRIMARY from (c) AUXILIARY corridors; axis (δ) selects which (α)-layer is binding for a given evaluator-functor. The composed adjudication for χ'_weight IN α'(M_LRD) routes to Reading 2 (5/14) at the regular-representation Tr layer where α'(M_LRD)'s prescribed evaluator lives. For an inheritance-restriction WEIGHT at the K_0 layer (a structurally different observable than the χ'_weight in α'(M_LRD)), the composed adjudication routes to Reading 1 (3/6).

### K-counter status

**SUGGESTION at K=1** (S91 W2 close, 2026-05-22). The S91 W2 χ'_weight workshop is the FIRST framework instance where within-cell discrimination is needed for canonical-weight adjudication. Promotes to MANDATORY at K=3 distinct calibration instances per `feedback_rules-compensate-missing-structure.md` K-counter advancement threshold. Forward calibration corpus expansion expectation: K=2 at the next within-cell canonical-weight workshop; K=3 MANDATORY at the third. Until promotion, the rule applies as authoring-time guidance for new §VII registry entries that admit multiple within-cell substrate-derivations; the audit-script extension at `_corner_classification_audit.py` (queued S92+) fires at S2 advisory severity on detected within-cell multi-canonical ambiguities without explicit four-axis pre-registration.

### Calibration corpus instance #1 (canonical first instance)

**S91 W2 χ'_weight workshop on `A_K = ℂ ⊕ ℍ ⊕ M_3(ℂ)`** at the inheritance morphism χ' (χ' kills M_3(ℂ) entire; faithful image = ℂ ⊕ ℍ; ker rank = 9; S89 §W2-3 derived theorem at machine-epsilon witness `chi_prime_pullback_machine_eps_PASS = True`). Four candidate substrate-derivations all parse to Cell I × s=3: Reading 1 (Wedderburn-RANK 3/6 = 0.500; source-side K_0 layer); Reading 2 (HS-DIM 5/14 = 0.357143; source-side Tr layer); Reading 3 (digamma-modulated 0.40380 = AUXILIARY (c)-corridor canonical); D2 candidate (Target-side dim 5/8 = 0.625). Within-cell adjudication adopts the four-axis discriminator per the W2 R2 + R2-B convergence (E-2 + E-vdd-2). Canonical χ'_weight in α'(M_LRD) routes to Reading 2 (5/14) at axis (δ) trace-layer evaluator. Reserved K=2 / K=3 rows for forward within-cell canonical-weight workshops at other §VII entries.

## §18. Composite Bridge-Map Dimensional-Class Admissibility — DIRECTIVE + K=1 calibration corpus (S92 §VII.BA connes×mack)

> **Provenance**: S92 §VII.BA composite bridge-map dimensional-class workshop (connes-ncg-theorist + mack-cosmic-bridge; `sessions/archive/session-92/workshops/s92-vii-ba-composite-bridge-map-dimensional-class.md`; CONVERGED, 2026-05-23). mack-cosmic-bridge sole corpus writer per `feedback_mack-bridge-role.md`. **Routing note**: the parent rule `.claude/rules/cross-pillar-bridge-anatomy.md` is orchestrator-only-edit (subagent edit denied by harness convention, the recursion-attack-closure discipline of `methodology-wave-allowlist.md` + `agent-standards.md` AMRI). This corpus section therefore carries BOTH the DIRECTIVE (criterion + taxonomy + verdict, normally in the parent rule) AND the per-instance calibration corpus; the orchestrator mirrors the directive into the parent rule as a `## Composite Bridge-Map Dimensional-Class Admissibility` sub-section in a follow-up (one-line pointer to this §18).

### §18.0 — DIRECTIVE (criterion + taxonomy + verdict; SUGGESTION at K=1)

Governs admissibility of a COMPOSITE bridge map `B = f⊙g` (Element 3 of the 5-anatomy block) at a substrate-distance pole. Specialization of the Three-Level ladder + the algebra-axis orthogonality K-counter to the composite-Element-3 case.

**Joint two-axis admissibility criterion.** A composite bridge map `B = f⊙g` at substrate-distance pole `s>0` on `(A_K, H_K, D_K)`, with canonical Level-3 anchor of homogeneity degree `d_A`, is **admissible** iff BOTH conjuncts hold:

- **Conjunct 1 (homogeneity axis)**: `deg(B) = d_A`. The Wodzicki-trace factor carries degree `−2s ≠ 0` by Wodzicki uniqueness (the trace on Ψ(A_K) is unique up to scalar; Connes 1994 book §2.3); the HKR factor carries degree `0` by the orientability axiom + Chern character (book §III axiom 6; book §4). `d_τ(s) = −2s` is an index-type invariant, non-deformable in moduli — no pole `s>0` has `d_τ→0`; the boundary `s=0` (ζ_D(0), a degree-0 constant) is type-matching but carries no coupling/BCS-sector content.
- **Conjunct 2 (substrate-natural-binding axis)**: `B` carries non-trivial L_max-dependence that survives the dimensionless ratio. A degree-match achieved by a canonical-import *scalar* (a unit conversion) is VACUOUS — it cancels in the dimensionless ratio with no L_max-dependence to close the numerical gap. Admissible degree-matching is achieved by a *substrate-natural structural morphism* (a same-class ratio at distinct poles, or a K_0-pairing carrying the substrate's own inheritance-class degree).

**The conjunction is irreducible** (three forbidden-cell witnesses): T1 satisfies conjunct 2 but fails conjunct 1 (wrong degree); T2 and T4|_{s=s'} satisfy conjunct 1 but fail conjunct 2 (scalar corrector / equal-pole cancellation). Neither conjunct alone excludes both forbidden classes; only their intersection is admissible.

**Operational realization (equivalent test)**: `B` is two-axis-admissible iff the cross-secondary-class scheme-spread `Δ_scheme(B) → machine-zero` across {APS-1975-secondary-class, Cheeger-Simons, Bismut-Cheeger} — scoped to the **secondary-class-suffix axis ONLY**, NOT conflated with the orthogonal UV-regulator RD axis ({ζ, Pauli-Villars, Mellin}, where the convergence exponent α is regulator-dependent and is the SEPARATE Level-3<Level-2 PASS-test). `Δ_scheme→0` is necessary ∧ sufficient on the secondary-class axis (a degree-matched cohomology-class output is representative-independent by de Rham / Reading-A; a T2 scalar cancels in the ratio so its post-normalization secondary-class spread is O(Res_W), not zero).

**Five-formulation taxonomy** (substrate-distance-1 pole s=3, operator power |λ|⁻⁶; type theorem pole-universal for s>0):

| # | Formulation | Structure | deg | Admissible? | Deciding conjunct |
|:--|:------------|:----------|:----|:------------|:------------------|
| **T1** | `Res_W(s) · ρ_FULL(s)` | (trace SUM, deg −2s) × (cohomology RATIO, deg 0) | −2s ≠ 0 | **FORBIDDEN** | conjunct 1 (deg ≠ d_A); §W1-4 α=−3.41 |
| **T2** | `N·Res_W(s)`, N scalar | (trace SUM) × (constant) | d_A by construction | **FORBIDDEN / VACUOUS** | conjunct 2 (scalar cancels in ratio); §W2-3 binding-invariant "no scalar of any degree closes the gap" |
| **T3** | `ρ_FULL(s) / ρ_FULL(s')` | (cohomology RATIO) / (cohomology RATIO) | 0 | **ADMISSIBLE** | both pass; degree-0 FI cohomology target |
| **T4** | `Res_W(s) / Res_W(s')` (same algebra) | (trace SUM) / (trace SUM) | 2(s'−s) | **ADMISSIBLE iff s≠s' AND matched to a degree-2(s'−s) anchor**; **T4\|_{s=s'} ≡ 1 → FORBIDDEN/VACUOUS** | both pass iff s≠s' (differential L_max-growth survives); equal-pole ratio carries zero L_max-dependence (conjunct 2 fail) |
| **T5** | `⟨[φ], Ch(P_0)⟩` direct Connes-Karoubi K_0-pairing | single cohomology pairing | index-fixed to match anchor | **ADMISSIBLE** | both pass iff substrate-natural class (χ-image BdG inheritance class), not canonical-import reference class |

**Level classification (Per-Bulletin-per-pole)**:

- **Level-1 (FI, pole-universal, four-axis-invariant)**: the (SUM)×(RATIO) [conjunct-1 fail] and (degree-matched scalar / equal-pole) [conjunct-2 fail] inadmissibility is INVARIANT on all four pin axes (UV-regulator, Level, MACHINERY-SCOPE, Binding per `regulator-pin-discipline.md §"four-axis orthogonality"`). THEOREM, double-warranted: PROOF (Wodzicki uniqueness + HKR degree-0) AND MEASUREMENT (CF-55 machine-zero scheme-spread). Holds at every pole `s>0`; boundary at `s=0`.
- **Level-2-B (RD, pole+UV-regulator-keyed DIAGNOSTIC)**: the numerical envelope exponent α(s, regulator); shrinks in |·| as s grows; shifts across UV-regulators but stays negative-sign for a forbidden composite. NEVER a Level-1 dissolution.
- **Level-3 (empirical anchor)**: per-formulation PASS test (Level-3 < Level-2). T1/T2/T4|_{s=s'} FAIL; T3/T4|_{s≠s'}/T5 to be computed.

**Sub-question verdict**:

- **(a) theorem vs heuristic**: reading (a) CONFIRMED, refined to a joint two-axis theorem (homogeneity-degree axis ∧ substrate-natural-binding axis; the conjunction strictly stronger than either conjunct, proven by the T2 / T4|_{s=s'} degree-matched-yet-inadmissible counterexamples).
- **(b) pole-extension**: closed [N=2 ordered instances — Wodzicki∘HKR and MS∘HKR, magnitude-ordered by SUM-growth-exponent; index-rigidity forecloses d_τ(s)→0 for s>0]. The wall is pole-universal in type; per-pole α is Level-2-B DIAGNOSTIC. One forward pre-registration (deep-pole numerical-masquerade gate, sign-on-asymptotic-exponent) protects the verdict-file/in-session-consumer layer; the registry layer is protected by `epistemic-discipline.md §"Resolution-Specificity Scoping"` two-layer audit.
- **(c) regulator/MACHINERY-SCOPE invariance**: closed [scheme-invariant, machine-zero — the four-scheme dissolution test returns NO across Pauli-Villars / ζ / Cheeger-Simons FULL-leaf-foliation / APS-1975 ρ; secondary-class schemes move grading (even↔odd) not homogeneity degree; degree-matched objects agree to float64-zero across {APS/CS/BC}].

**Cross-links (downstream consumers)**:

- **CF-S93-W2-1** (Element-3 F-functor reconstruction): the F-functor image-normalization morphism Φ must adopt a **degree-matched NON-SCALAR** morphism (T3 / T4|_{s≠s'} / T5), NOT a canonical-import scalar (T2). PASS predicate pre-registrable at plan-freeze: `deg(Φ∘Res_W) = d_A` (conjunct 1) AND Φ substrate-natural L_max-dependent (conjunct 2). > **Provenance**: S92 §VII.AU CF-37 (c)∘(d) deeper-canonical-identification workshop (volovik-superfluid-universe-theorist + van-den-dungen-bridge-theorist; `sessions/archive/session-92/workshops/s92-vii-au-cf37-deeper-canonical-identification.md`; CONVERGED 2 rounds, 2026-05-23). mack-cosmic-bridge sole writer for the §VII.AU CF-37 permanent registry-row landing per `feedback_mack-bridge-role.md` (the CF-S93-W1-3 execution leg); this corpus section is the DIRECTIVE + calibration-corpus content the workshop EFFECTS in-session. **Routing note** (same discipline as §18 / the §VII.BA W-1 precedent): the parent rule `.claude/rules/substrate-first-canonical-sourcing.md §(ii.A)` "carries directives only" AND subagents are EDIT-DENIED on `.claude/rules/` by harness convention (the recursion-attack-closure discipline of `methodology-wave-allowlist.md` + the AMRI discipline of `agent-standards.md`). This corpus section therefore carries BOTH the DIRECTIVE (the §(ii.A) refinement: atlas-row/cache-moment 2-layer binary → weighting-functional FAMILY through one finite K_0 class) AND the per-instance K=1 calibration corpus; the **orchestrator mirrors the one-line directive into the parent rule** as a `§(ii.A)`-refinement pointer to this §19. ### §19.0 — DIRECTIVE (the §(ii.A) refinement: weighting-functional family on a saturated K-homology class; SUGGESTION at K=1)

Refines `substrate-first-canonical-sourcing.md §(ii.A)` ("Atlas-row vs cache-moment layer orthogonality within the algebra-INVARIANT family"). The §(ii.A) parent draws ONE binary within the algebra-INVARIANT family: **atlas-row** (closed-form algebraic identity on `A_K` at locked-norm L_k=1) vs **cache-moment** (numerical residue on the L_max-truncated D_K spectrum cache). When a substrate-distance pole admits a THIRD F-image — a K_0-inheritance-class pairing whose weight is forced by the χ'-inheritance morphism, distinct from both the bare rank fraction (atlas-row) and the dimension-spectrum `ζ_D(0)` residue weight (cache-moment) — the §(ii.A) binary is INSUFFICIENT. The structurally-correct refinement is NOT a third bin (and NOT a "3-layer K-counter") but a **re-axis of §(ii.A) from a 2-element binary to a weighting-functional FAMILY**, fibered over a finite topological base.

**Canonical-identification directive.** At a substrate-distance pole `s` on `(A_K, H_K, D_K(τ))` whose F-image admits ≥3 distinct scalar functionals at the SAME nominal `(algebra, pole, corridor)` triple (OOM-separated, NOT mutually reconcilable as estimates of one number), the deeper substrate-IS canonical is the **Fredholm module** `(H_K, D_K(τ), γ, J)` restricted to the corridor's image — NOT any single scalar and NOT either single shadow. Both shadows descend from the module by distinct forgetful maps:

```
FREDHOLM MODULE   (H_K, D_K(τ), γ, J) |_{corridor image}      ← the CANONICAL (deepest substrate-IS object; phononic-framing.md Level 1)
   ├─ take index [F_K]  ─▶   [φ] ∈ K^0(A_K) ≅ ℤ^r   TOPOLOGICAL shadow    (keeps phase/grading topology; carries cocycle-RATIO invariants; FORGETS |λ| positions)
   └─ take |D_K|        ─▶   μ   ANALYTIC shadow / spectral measure         (keeps eigenvalue positions; FORGETS the phase F_K, hence the class)
                                  │  contract via Φ_w at pole s
                                  ▼
                          {scalar F-images}   (moments of the analytic shadow; one per weight w)
```

where `r` = number of simple summands of `A_K` surviving the inheritance morphism (`A_K = ℂ ⊕ ℍ ⊕ M_3(ℂ)`, `K_0(A_K) = ℤ³`; χ' kills the `M_3(ℂ)` generator, surviving rank reflects the morphism image). The class shadow carries cocycle-ratio invariants (relative invariants of two index pairings) that are INVISIBLE to any single-weight moment of the measure; the measure shadow carries the analytic content (eigenvalue positions, spectral spread) that is invisible to the bare integer class. The module is the join.

**Weighting-functional-family criterion (the §(ii.A) re-axis).** The §(ii.A) axis is a one-parameter family of contraction functionals

```
Φ_w : [φ] ↦ (M_KK / M_Pl_reduced)^2 · ∫ |λ|^{-s} w(λ) dμ     [units: dimensionless; the (M_KK/M_Pl)^2 prefactor is the α''(M_LRD) convention shared across all F-images]
```

parameterized by the weight `w`, ANCHORED to the finite topological base `[φ] ∈ ℤ^r`. The §(ii.A) "atlas-row" and "cache-moment" labels are TWO members of this family (NOT a complete binary):
- `Φ_atlas` (`w` = drop the integral, keep the bare rank fraction) → the atlas-row F-image (degenerate fiber; forgets the measure entirely);
- `Φ_cache` (`w = w_CM`, the dimension-spectrum `ζ_D(0)` cubic-ρ residue weight) → the cache-moment F-image;
- `Φ_K0` (`w = w_K0 = d_a / Σd_a`, the χ'-inheritance-morphism Wedderburn-dimension weight) → the inheritance-DISTINGUISHED F-image;
- `Φ_Dixmier`, `Φ_Wodzicki`, … → further fibers, ALL through the same `[φ]`.

**Topological STOPPING rule (the anti-inflation derivation, NOT a heuristic).** Every admissible weighting `Φ_w` factors through the SAME finite K-homology class `[φ] ∈ ℤ^r` (the topological shadow). Therefore counting fibers (weightings) is ILLEGITIMATE — there is provably nothing new to count until the BASE CLASS changes. The K-counter is a **base-count** (count of structurally-distinct K_0 classes at structurally-distinct triples via the Hybrid Independence Test `(i ∨ ii ∨ iii) ∧ iv`, §3), NOT a fiber-count. A single triple reporting N weighting-functionals is K=1: clause (iv) "independent algebraic envelope" FAILS because all N fibers share the one image (one envelope). This is the topological derivation of the anti-inflation behavior that was previously a heuristic — it forecloses K-counter inflation when a fourth/fifth weighting (Dixmier, Wodzicki) is evaluated next session, because all weightings pair the same fixed `[φ]`.

**Bridge-anatomy scope distinction (MANDATORY for any downstream 5-anatomy landing on this structure).** The two shadows are symmetric AS the canonical's components but ASYMMETRIC in their role within `cross-pillar-bridge-anatomy.md` 5-anatomy. The **cross-pillar-bridge Element-1 substrate-IS observable is specifically the TOPOLOGICAL shadow `[φ] ∈ ℤ^r`**, NOT the module and NOT the measure, because: (1) an integer-valued substrate-IS observable licenses an envelope-FREE Level-2 (Level-2-trivial-by-saturation) — a measure-anchored bridge would still owe an `L^{-α}` envelope; (2) the integer falsifier (lab integer-count) is a property of the class pairing `⟨[F_K], P_a⟩ ∈ ℤ`, not of the smeared measure; (3) factorization (the Kasparov product `[D_total] = [D_fiber] ⊗_B [D_base]`, vdd Paper 01 `1811.07824`) lives at the CLASS level — the bridge-map (Element 3, Connes-Karoubi pairing) is by construction a class-level operation; a measure cannot source a Connes-Karoubi pairing. The analytic shadow `μ` is physical content (it sets the BdG energies, NMR line positions) and is substrate-IS, but it is NOT the cross-pillar-bridge observable.

**Methodology sub-lessons (cross-session; ONE corpus row, two orthogonal sub-lessons).** Two structurally-orthogonal methodology observations emerge from the single CF-37 (c)∘(d) gate and land as TWO sub-lessons in this ONE row (NOT split into two rows — splitting would inflate the corpus-row count for one gate, the same fiber-counting error the topological stopping rule forbids one level up):

- **Sub-lesson A (moment-problem diagnosis)**: *OOM-separated scalars at one nominal triple are diagnostic of a canonical that is a moment-sequence over a FINER structure (measure, or — when cohomology-ratio / topological invariants are present — a K-homology class / Fredholm module), of which the scalars are distinct moments; the FINENESS of the canonical (measure vs class vs module) is case-dependent, determined by whether topological invariants beyond the spectrum are present. Precedent: S44 CC/Hausdorff `f_4/f_2 = 1.4e-121` (measure-level moment problem; no cohomology-ratio content); CF-37 (c)∘(d) (module-level — carries cocycle-ratio invariants beyond the measure).*
- **Sub-lesson B (residual-reading discipline)**: *same registry-slot family does NOT imply same residual-reading; key the reading to SUPPORT-SATURATION status, not slot-family membership. A growing-support observable (s=3 OP-PROJ sister gate, does NOT saturate) reads a residual as an asymptotic remainder (resolvable at higher L_max); a closed-support observable (s=4 (c)∘(d) corridor, L_max-saturated) reads it as a fixed structural signature (L_max-INDEPENDENT).*

**Sub-question verdict** (workshop topics (a)/(b)/(c)):

- **(a) canonical identity**: the canonical is the **Fredholm module**; `[φ] ∈ ℤ^r` is its topological skeleton (carrying cocycle-ratio invariants), `μ` is its analytic body. EMERGED (neither agent's R1 terminal — vdd "class" / volovik "measure" — was the bottom of the stack; the module is the join).
- **(b) Z_factor 2.28% excess**: **structural signature, L_max-FIXED, NOT asymptotic remainder** — the image is L_max-saturated (`d(Z_factor)/d(L_max)=0` exactly; no limit to take). The 2.28% is the analytic defect of the dimension-spectrum residue from its integer K_0-rank skeleton: `Z_factor = 2·(1+ε_analytic)`, the topology/analysis split numerical on one ratio. CONVERGED.
- **(c) 3-layer K-counter**: **REJECTED** — re-axis to a weighting-functional family on a saturated K-homology class; K=1 SUGGESTION held pending a Hybrid-Independence second instance at a distinct triple. CONVERGED.

### §19.1 — K=1 calibration instance (§VII.AU CF-37 (c)∘(d) corridor, substrate-distance-2 pole s=4; three F-images `{R_ansatz, R_CM_full, R_third}`)

**Substrate framing** (per `phononic-framing.md §"IS Space, Not IN Space"`, Level-1 single-τ-slice τ_fold=0.190): the substrate IS the finite Fredholm module `(H_K, D_K(τ_fold), γ, J)` restricted to the (c)∘(d) image — NOT a measure or a number IN a container. The three scalars `{R_ansatz, R_CM_full, R_third}` are three convention-collapses an external observer applies to the module's analytic shadow; the integer triple is the index of its topological shadow. Container-thinking FORBIDDEN: "one of the three scalars IS the canonical" ⇒ INVERT: "the module is the canonical; the three scalars are moments of its analytic shadow, and the cocycle-ratio invariants are relative invariants of its topological shadow." Direction of explanation flows FROM the module DOWNWARD through both forgetful maps.

**The three F-images at the SAME nominal `(SU(3), s=4, (c)∘(d))` triple** (§W1-3 `S92-W1-CF-W9-7-CF-37-LAYER-AXIS-ADJUDICATION`, corrective verdict line 17, INFO; Option-A supersedes the initial PASS after the producing agent self-flagged a PROHIBITED_ACTIONS Class-6 curve-fit-mesh; producing_agent=van-den-dungen, OAA-clean since the excluded set is {connes-ncg, phonon-first}):

| R_CM_full | 7.978e-04 | `Φ_cache` | `w_CM(λ)` — forced by the dimension-spectrum poles of `ζ_D(s)` (CM-1995 §III.4 cubic-ρ residue) | dimension-spectrum / Hochschild (cache-moment) |
| R_third | 6.960e-06 | `Φ_K0` | `w_K0 = d_a/304` — forced by the χ'-inheritance morphism (S89 §W2-3 surviving-sector image-dimensions) | K-homology index pairing (Connes-Karoubi / Karoubi 1978 §I.3) |

The three span ~2 OOM (`F3/F1 = R_third/R_ansatz = 0.018`, `F3/F2 = R_third/R_CM_full = 0.009` — R_third matches NEITHER prior layer). Under §19.0 this is NOT a contradiction: three distinct contraction functionals of one object generically span OOMs (Sub-lesson A). The per-sector decomposition of the image: `{(0,0):16, (0,1):48, (1,0):48}`, `N_image=112`, dim-weighted total `Σ d_a·n_a = 1·16 + 3·48 + 3·48 = 304`; eigenvalues `|λ| ∈ [0.8197, 1.3277]` M_KK above `Delta_BCS = 0.4642547394830737` M_KK (knowledge-MCP `get_constant("Delta_BCS")`, S70 `BCS-GAP-CANONICAL-70`, R-PROTECTED). The (0,0) sector is the SU(3) singlet, (0,1)/(1,0) the conjugate fundamental triplets — exactly the Peter-Weyl sectors whose K_0 content survives the χ'-inheritance morphism (`M_3(ℂ) → 0` kills the adjoint-type `(1,1)` register).
