## §21. Publication-Precision Tolerance + DEFERRED-to-Substrate-Recompute Tagging for Element-5 Anchors — DIRECTIVE + K=1 calibration corpus (S92 §VII.AY connes×mack)

> **Slot-rerouting note** (per `epistemic-discipline.md §"Registry-Write Hygiene under Parallel-Writer Race"` item 3): this section was authored as §20 but rerouted to §21 at landing-time because a concurrent S92 workshop (§VII.AX.OP-PROJ JE5 connes×volovik, Level-3 Annotation Discipline) landed its own `## §20.` at this corpus's same write-window. The next-free-section scan detected the collision (two `## §20.` headers); this section (mack-cosmic-bridge sole writer, §VII.AY) reroutes to the next free number §21, leaving the §VII.AX §20 in place. No content lost; all internal §-references in this section updated to §21.

> **Provenance**: S92 §VII.AY cocycle-ratio ‖φ_67‖/‖φ_88‖ F1-vs-F2 canonical-identity workshop (connes-ncg-theorist + mack-cosmic-bridge; `sessions/archive/session-92/workshops/s92-vii-ay-cocycle-ratio-f1-vs-f2.md`; CONVERGED 2 rounds, 2026-05-23). mack-cosmic-bridge sole corpus writer per `feedback_mack-bridge-role.md`. **Routing note** (same discipline as §18 / the §VII.BA W-1 precedent and §19 / the §VII.AU W-2 precedent): the parent rules `.claude/rules/epistemic-discipline.md §"Publication-Precision Pre-Registration (Class 8.3)"` AND `.claude/rules/cross-pillar-bridge-anatomy.md` (Element-5 audit) are orchestrator-only-edit — subagents are EDIT-DENIED on `.claude/rules/` by harness convention (the recursion-attack-closure discipline of `methodology-wave-allowlist.md` + the AMRI discipline of `agent-standards.md`). This corpus section therefore carries BOTH the DIRECTIVE (the Class-8.3 publication-precision tolerance rule + the DEFERRED-tagging anti-laundering discipline + the two-layer Stage-3 separation, normally extending the parent rules) AND the per-instance K=1 calibration corpus; the **orchestrator mirrors the one-line directive into the parent rules** as Class-8.3 extension pointers to this §21. The verbatim parent-rule mirror text the orchestrator should land is given at the END of §21.0 (ORCHESTRATOR-RESERVED block).

### §21.0 — DIRECTIVE (publication-precision tolerance + DEFERRED tag + two-layer Stage-3; SUGGESTION at K=1)

Extends `epistemic-discipline.md §"Publication-Precision Pre-Registration (Class 8.3)"` items 2 + 6 (downstream-verifier `rel_tol ≥ 10^(−publication_sig_figs)`; sanity-check `abs_tol ≥ pin_value · 10^(−publication_sig_figs)`) to the cross-pillar-bridge Element-5 Stage-2/Stage-3 verification case, AND closes the floor-precision-over-reading laundering pathway that the original false-arithmetic gloss (`Fraction(793346,108307) = 7.324992 "Sage-exact"`, arithmetically false — `0.793346/0.108307 = 7.3249743783873615`) instantiated one layer down.

**Three structural rules (the DIRECTIVE):**

**(R1) Publication-precision tolerance rule (the ABSOLUTE-vs-RELATIVE + agreement-floor sharpening).** When a cross-pillar-bridge Element-5 anchor is a substrate-IS quantity published at `n` significant figures, its Stage-2/Stage-3 verifier tolerance MUST be:

```
rel_tol ≥ 10^(−sig_figs_of_AGREEMENT)        [RELATIVE, not absolute]
```

where `sig_figs_of_agreement` is the number of significant figures to which the candidate F-images of the anchor mutually agree — which may be STRICTLY FEWER than `n` (the per-image publication precision). An ABSOLUTE tolerance `|x − pin| < ε_abs` on a quantity of magnitude `M` is a RELATIVE tolerance `ε_abs/M`; if `ε_abs/M < 10^(−n)` the verifier is PIN-TIGHT-SOURCE-LOOSE (Class-8.3) and is structurally guaranteed to FAIL at the publication-precision boundary, NOT at the substrate-physics boundary. The two-count failure mode to check: (i) ABSOLUTE where it should be RELATIVE; (ii) even read relatively, the floor `10^(−n)` over-claims `n`-sf agreement when the genuine inter-image agreement is fewer than `n` sig figs (a rounding-slip on the agreement-precision claim). The agreement precision is computed by `round_to_ksf` comparison across images, NOT assumed equal to the per-image publication precision.

**(R2) DEFERRED-to-substrate-recompute tagging discipline (the anti-laundering safeguard — E3).** Any Stage-2/Stage-3 verdict whose PASS holds at the publication-precision floor — and therefore CANNOT discriminate between the candidate F-images of its anchor (the re-toleranced PASS is near-tautological at the agreement floor) — MUST carry a tag

```
value=...canonical-value-question-DEFERRED-to-<substrate-recompute-CF>...
```

in its verdict-line `value=` field, so no downstream consumer reads the floor-level PASS as adjudicating a sub-floor significant figure. This is the structural generalization of the false-gloss lesson (a floor-precision quantity read as carrying a sub-floor digit), caught one layer up at the verdict-emission layer. Absent the tag, a floor-level PASS is structurally indistinguishable from a substrate-resolving PASS — the exact over-reading the false gloss committed.

**(R3) Two-layer Stage-3 separation (the promotion-hygiene principle — E1).** A STAGE-1-CANDIDATE cross-pillar-bridge / joint theorem may be blocked by TWO structurally independent obstructions, cleared at different layers by different actions:

```
Obstruction 1 (audit-tolerance layer):  Element-5 Stage-2 verifier tolerance < publication floor.
   Cleared by:  re-pre-register rel_tol ≥ 10^(−sig_figs_of_agreement) RELATIVE (R1).
   Clears:      Stage-3 ELIGIBILITY (the 3-axis PASS-AND).     Substrate question resolved: NONE.

Obstruction 2 (canonical-pin layer):    pin holds a methodology-floor F-image, not the substrate-first source.
   Cleared by:  re-pin to the bit-exact substrate computation (the substrate-first canonical).
   Clears:      Stage-3-PERMANENT PROMOTION (the tag-flip).     Substrate question resolved: historiography.
```

Stage-3 eligibility rides on the tolerance matching publication precision; Stage-3-PERMANENT rides on the pin being substrate-sourced. Conflating the two (reading the precision-floor mismatch as a substrate obstruction) blocks the whole promotion when only the tolerance layer is at fault.

**(R4) DEFERRED-hold interim-pin discipline (the substrate-first interim state — E4).** When (i) the terminal canonical of a pin is a pending substrate-recompute (the substrate-first source not yet evaluated), AND (ii) every LIVE consumer of the pin is insensitive to the interim value (the sensitive consumer, if any, is re-toleranced to the publication floor under which all candidate F-images pass), THEN the substrate-first-correct interim state is to HOLD the current value under an explicit `DEFERRED-to-<recompute-CF>` tag — NOT to interim-re-pin to the on-record-direction F-image. A sideways re-pin between two methodology-floor images (even toward the on-record direction) is a mini-convention-shop per `substrate-first-canonical-sourcing.md §(i)`: it freezes an image the recompute will overwrite, spends a consumer cascade for zero correctness, and erases the open-question marker. The on-record direction is *historiography* (which prior image was faithful); the interim pin is *what value sits in the canonical pin while the substrate is computed* — and for the latter, no methodology-floor image is canonical. R2 prevents over-READING a floor-precision PASS (verdict-emission layer); R4 prevents over-WRITING a pending pin (canonical-constants layer); together they close both laundering paths.

**Status**: SUGGESTION at K=1. Promotes to MANDATORY at K=3 distinct calibration instances per `feedback_rules-compensate-missing-structure.md`. K=2/K=3 advancement candidates: any future STAGE-1-CANDIDATE cross-pillar-bridge / joint theorem whose Element-5 anchor is a published-precision substrate-IS quantity blocked solely by an over-tight Stage-2 tolerance (R1/R3), on a structurally-distinct (algebra, anchor, pole) triple per the Hybrid Independence Test (§3). Audit: `computations/_shared/_cross_pillar_bridge_audit.py::detect_element5_publication_precision_tolerance` (S2 advisory at K=1; NOT HARD-HALT) — flags an Element-5 Stage-2 verifier whose tolerance is tighter than the anchor's publication-precision floor, and flags a floor-level PASS missing the R2 DEFERRED tag.

> **ORCHESTRATOR-RESERVED — verbatim parent-rule mirror text** (the orchestrator lands these as extension-pointers in `.claude/rules/epistemic-discipline.md §"Publication-Precision Pre-Registration (Class 8.3)"` AND `.claude/rules/cross-pillar-bridge-anatomy.md` Element-5 audit; subagents are edit-denied on `.claude/rules/`):
>
> *"**(Class-8.3 Element-5 extension — publication-precision tolerance + DEFERRED tag + two-layer Stage-3; S92 §VII.AY; SUGGESTION at K=1)**: a cross-pillar-bridge Element-5 anchor published at `n` sig figs MUST have its Stage-2/Stage-3 verifier tolerance set RELATIVE at `rel_tol ≥ 10^(−sig_figs_of_agreement)`, where the agreement precision (computed by `round_to_ksf` comparison across the anchor's candidate F-images) may be STRICTLY FEWER than `n`; an ABSOLUTE `<ε` tolerance with `ε/pin < 10^(−n)` is PIN-TIGHT-SOURCE-LOOSE (structurally FAIL at the precision floor, not the physics boundary). A Stage-2/Stage-3 PASS that holds only at the publication-precision floor (and therefore cannot discriminate between the anchor's candidate F-images) MUST carry `canonical-value-question-DEFERRED-to-<substrate-recompute-CF>` in its verdict-line `value=` field (anti-laundering: no downstream consumer may read a floor-level PASS as adjudicating a sub-floor digit). Stage-3 separates into ELIGIBILITY (cleared by the tolerance fix; no substrate question resolved) and STAGE-3-PERMANENT (cleared by re-pinning to the bit-exact substrate-first canonical). During the pending-recompute interim, HOLD the current pin value under a `DEFERRED-to-<recompute-CF>` tag rather than sideways-re-pinning to the on-record-direction F-image (a methodology-floor image is never canonical). Full directive + K=1 calibration corpus: `sessions/framework/registry/cross-pillar-bridge-corpus.md §21`."*

### §21.1 — K=1 calibration instance (§VII.AY.OP-PROJ Element-5 cocycle ratio `R = (δE_6·δE_7)/(δE_8)²`; F1 = 7.324974 vs F2 = 7.324992; substrate-distance M_3(ℂ) Cartan/chiral cohomology pairing)

**Substrate framing** (per `phononic-framing.md §"IS Space, Not IN Space"`, Level-1 single-τ-slice τ_fold=0.190): the substrate IS the single Hochschild cohomology-class pairing `R = ‖[φ_67]‖/‖[φ_88]‖ = (δE_6·δE_7)/(δE_8)²` on `(A_K, H_K, D_K(τ_fold))|_{M_3(ℂ)}` — one eigenvalue-gap quantity, NOT a number IN a container. F1 = Fraction(793346,108307) = 7.3249743783873615, F2 = Fraction(114453,15625) = 7.324992, and the canonical pin are three methodology-floor F-images under `F : substrate → methodology → audit`. Container-thinking FORBIDDEN: "one of the decimals IS the canonical" ⇒ INVERT: "the gap quantity is the canonical; the decimals are F-images, and the dispute over their 6th digit is about how faithfully an image tracks the gap — never about the substrate, which is Morita-invariant and was never in dispute." Direction of explanation flows FROM the gap quantity DOWNWARD through the F-images.

**The three F-images at the SAME nominal `(M_3(ℂ), cocycle pairing)` substrate quantity** (verified live via knowledge-MCP `get_constant`, 2026-05-23):

| F-image | Value | Arithmetic origin | Provenance status on disk |
|:--------|:------|:------------------|:--------------------------|
| F1 | 7.3249743783873615 | direct quotient of the published 6-sf norms `cocycle_norm_phi67=0.793346` / `cocycle_norm_phi88=0.108307` (both `get_constant`, `Superseded: False`); bit-identical to `Fraction(793346,108307)` | the image of the recorded provenance under the recorded operation (norm ratio) |
| F2 | 7.324992 EXACTLY | `Fraction(114453,15625)`; `15625 = 5^6`; the reduced form of `7324992/10^6` (the published 6-sf decimal) | the integer `7324992` appears NOWHERE in the record except as the published decimal; the prior `= 0.793346/0.108307 (Sage-exact)` gloss is arithmetically FALSE |
| canonical pin | 7.324992 | `substrate_cocycle_ratio_67_88` (`get_constant`: value 7.324992, gate `S86-W5-CANON-EXTRACT`, `Superseded: False`) | holds F2's VALUE under a norm-ratio OPERATION-provenance that yields F1 — the on-disk Class-8.3 value/operation mismatch |

`cross-mult residual: 793346·15625 − 114453·108307 = −29,821 ≠ 0 ⇒ F1 ≠ F2 in ℚ`; `Δ_abs = |F1 − F2| = 1.762161e-5`; `Δ_rel = 2.405684e-6`. Agreement precision: `round_to_6sf(F1) = 7.32497 ≠ 7.32499 = round_to_6sf(F2)`; `round_to_5sf(F1) = 7.3250 = round_to_5sf(F2)` — they agree to **5** sig figs, NOT 6 (the §W7-1 Step-7 "6-sf agreement" prose is a rounding slip; the corrected `canonical_constants.py:277` comment already carries the 5-sf statement).

**The R1 Class-8.3 verdict (the §W7-2 `<1e-6` tolerance).** §W7-2 Axis-B-primary tested `|F1 − canonical_pin| ≤ 1e-6` ABSOLUTE. At pin ≈ 7.32, implied `rel_tol = 1e-6/7.32 = 1.366e-7` — ~7.3× tighter than the publication floor `10^(−6)` for a 6-sf-sourced quantity (PIN-TIGHT-SOURCE-LOOSE, count (i): ABSOLUTE where it should be RELATIVE). Sharpened by the 5-sf agreement (count (ii)): the principled floor is `rel_tol ≥ 10^(−5) = 1e-5` RELATIVE. Under `rel_tol = 1e-5`: `Δ_rel = 2.406e-6 < 1e-5` ⇒ §W7-2 Axis-B-primary PASS; combined with the already-PASSing Axis-A (vdd) + Axis-B (cross-pillar spectral-geometer, both at the substrate-IS structural ceiling), the 3-axis Stage-2 PASS-ANDs. The §W7-2 FAIL (verdict `2018915e`) and §W7-7 FAIL (`2ca01729`) are BOTH precision-floor artifacts at this one over-tight tolerance — the Hochschild-Künneth Morita-Invariance theorem was never in question. Class-8.3, independent of the F1-vs-F2 winner (the chain holds for any faithful image of a 6-sf-sourced anchor).

**The R2 DEFERRED-tag necessity (why the re-toleranced PASS is near-tautological).** Under `rel_tol = 1e-5`, BOTH F1 and F2 pass against the current pin `7.324992` (they agree to 5 sf), so the re-toleranced Stage-3-eligibility PASS is diagnostically agnostic to the 6th digit — it confirms only that a 5-sf-grade anchor passes a 5-sf-grade test, NOT that the canonical value is `7.324992`. Recorded without the tag, a future gate could cite "§VII.AY Stage-3 PASS confirms 7.324992" — the same over-reading as the original "Sage-exact" gloss, one layer up. §VII.AY is the INAUGURAL instance where the framework explicitly tags a floor-level PASS as `canonical-value-question-DEFERRED-to-R_machine-recompute (CF-S93-W7-1)`.

### §19.2 — Module-as-canonical PERMANENT registry-row landing (CF-S93-W1-3 EXECUTION leg; value-pinned [φ_cd]; S93 §W2-4 mack-cosmic-bridge)

> **Provenance**: S93 §W2-4 `S93-W2-4-VII-AU-CF37-MODULE-AS-CANONICAL-CORPUS-ROW` (mack-cosmic-bridge sole writer per `feedback_mack-bridge-role.md`; single-shot AFTER-pattern per `registry-landing.md §"Bridge-Landing Script Architecture"`, 2026-05-24). **The canonical IS the Fredholm module.** At the §VII.AU CF-37 (c)∘(d) corridor on `(A_K, H_K, D_K(τ))` (substrate-distance-2 pole s=4), the deeper substrate-IS canonical is the **Fredholm module** `(H_K, D_K(τ_fold=0.190), γ, J)|_{(c)∘(d) image}` — a Level-1 single-τ-slice object per `phononic-framing.md §"IS Space, Not IN Space"` — NOT any single scalar and NOT either single shadow. Both shadows descend from the module by distinct forgetful maps (index → class; `|D_K|` → measure), exactly as the §19.0 directive diagram specifies. Direction of explanation flows FROM the module DOWNWARD; container-thinking ("one of the three scalars `{R_ansatz, R_CM_full, R_third}` IS the canonical") is FORBIDDEN and INVERTED to "the module is the canonical; the three scalars are moments of its analytic shadow; the cocycle-ratio invariants are relative invariants of its topological shadow".

**Content predicate (i) — TOPOLOGICAL SHADOW `[φ_cd] ∈ K^0(A_K) ≅ ℤ³` as the cross-pillar-bridge Element-1 substrate-IS observable.** **VALUE-PINNED** to `[φ_cd] = (n_(0,0), n_(0,1), n_(1,0)) = (0, 0, 0) ∈ ℤ³` (S93 §W2-1 `S93-W2-1-VII-AU-CF37-FREDHOLM-INDEX-INTEGER-TRIPLE`, audit_sha256=`76e5d744b36b7b35edced48bffe63659c0e667ee2f60bd9272203819496c5f99`; the integer-triple value loaded from `computations/session-93/s93_w2_1_vii_au_cf37_fredholm_index_integer_triple.npz` key `phi_cd_integer_triple`). This DISCHARGES the §19.1 honest residual "value-pinning queued": the per-sector indices are now COMPUTED (machine-zero integrality, `max_a |n_a − round(n_a)| = 0.00e+00`), not existence-argued. The cross-pillar-bridge Element-1 substrate-IS observable is a CONCRETE integer triple ⇒ envelope-FREE Level-2 (Level-2-trivial-by-saturation: the image is L_max-saturated on the closed (c)∘(d) corridor, `N_image=112` bit-identical at L=10/12, foreclosing any `L^{-α}`). The per-sector eigenvalue counts `{(0,0):16, (0,1):48, (1,0):48}`, `N_image=112`, dim-weighted total `Σ d_a·n_a = 1·16 + 3·48 + 3·48 = 304` are the substrate-IS image data; the integer triple `[φ_cd]` is the index of its topological shadow (a 48-mode sector carries index 0 via a 24/24 grading split — the answer the §19.1 open question awaited, now resolved by W2-1: **the 24/24 split, index 0** per sector, `dim H_a^+ = dim H_a^- = d_a·8`).

**Content predicate (ii) — ANALYTIC SHADOW `μ_cd` is physical-content-NOT-bridge-observable (scope distinction, corpus §19.0 MANDATORY).** The analytic shadow `μ_cd` (= `|D_K|` restricted to the image, the spectral measure) carries the BdG energies and NMR line positions the laboratory measures, and it IS substrate-IS, but it is **NOT the cross-pillar-bridge Element-1 observable**. Three structural reasons (corpus §19.0): (1) an integer-valued substrate-IS observable licenses an envelope-FREE Level-2, whereas a measure-anchored bridge would still owe an `L^{-α}` envelope; (2) the integer falsifier (lab integer-count) is a property of the class pairing `⟨[F_K], P_a⟩ ∈ ℤ`, not of the smeared measure; (3) the Connes-Karoubi bridge-map (Element 3) is by construction a class-level operation — a measure cannot source a Connes-Karoubi pairing (Kasparov-product factorization `[D_total] = [D_fiber] ⊗_B [D_base]`, vdd Paper 01 `1811.07824`, lives at the CLASS level). Neither shadow dominates ⇒ canonical = the MODULE (the join).

**Content predicate (iii) — WEIGHTING-FUNCTIONAL FAMILY `Φ_w` + topological STOPPING rule (base-count not fiber-count) at K=1 SUGGESTION.** The `substrate-first-canonical-sourcing.md §(ii.A)` atlas-row/cache-moment binary is re-axed to a one-parameter **weighting-functional family** `Φ_w : [φ_cd] ↦ (M_KK / M_Pl_reduced)² · ∫ |λ|^{-s} w(λ) dμ`, parameterized by the weight `w` and ANCHORED to the finite topological base `[φ_cd] ∈ ℤ³`. **Content predicate (iv) — TWO methodology sub-lessons in ONE row (NOT split into two — the fiber-counting error one level up).** Two structurally-orthogonal methodology observations emerge from the single CF-37 (c)∘(d) gate and land as TWO sub-lessons in THIS ONE row (splitting them into two rows would inflate the corpus-row count for one gate — the same fiber-counting error the topological STOPPING rule forbids one level up):

- **Sub-lesson B (residual-reading discipline)**: same registry-slot family does NOT imply same residual-reading; key the reading to SUPPORT-SATURATION status, not slot-family membership. A growing-support observable (s=3 OP-PROJ sister gate, does NOT saturate, rel_drift=2.374e-3) reads a residual as an asymptotic remainder (resolvable at higher L_max); a closed-support observable (s=4 (c)∘(d) corridor, L_max-saturated) reads it as a fixed structural signature (L_max-INDEPENDENT, e.g. the Z_factor 2.28%).

`HIT = (YES ∨ YES ∨ YES) ∧ YES = YES`. Clauses (i)/(ii)/(iii) are each independently sufficient via the disjunction; clause (iv) is the load-bearing conjunct the §19.0 directive named (independent algebraic envelope on the rank-4 triple).

## §20. Level-3 Annotation Discipline — DIRECTIVE + K=1 calibration corpus (S92 §VII.AX.OP-PROJ JE5 connes×volovik)

> **Provenance**: S92 §VII.AX.OP-PROJ JOINT Element-5 central-value-vs-conjunctive workshop (connes-ncg-theorist + volovik-superfluid-universe-theorist; `sessions/archive/session-92/workshops/s92-vii-ax-op-proj-je5-central-vs-conjunctive.md`; CONVERGED 2 rounds, 2026-05-23). volovik-superfluid-universe-theorist landed this corpus section as the final-turn EFFECTED-IN-SESSION deliverable; mack-cosmic-bridge is sole writer for the §VII.AX.OP-PROJ permanent registry-text Eq. (2′) correction (the housekeeping §A execution leg). **Routing note** (same discipline as §18 / §19 / the §VII.BA W-1 + §VII.AU W-2 precedents): the parent rules `.claude/rules/cross-pillar-bridge-anatomy.md §"Registry-PASS criterion"` AND `.claude/rules/registry-landing.md` are orchestrator-only-edit (subagents EDIT-DENIED on `.claude/rules/` by harness convention — the recursion-attack-closure discipline of `methodology-wave-allowlist.md` + the AMRI discipline of `agent-standards.md`). This corpus section therefore carries BOTH the DIRECTIVE (the three-clause Level-3 annotation discipline + the Class-(i) detector predicate, normally mirrored into the parent rules) AND the per-instance K=1 calibration corpus; the **orchestrator mirrors the directive** into `cross-pillar-bridge-anatomy.md §"Registry-PASS criterion"` (the three-sentence sub-clause) AND `registry-landing.md` (the Class-(i) detector registration). The verbatim ORCHESTRATOR-RESERVED mirror text is given at the END of §20.0.

### §20.0 — DIRECTIVE (the three-clause Level-3 annotation discipline + Class-(i) detector; SUGGESTION at K=1)

Specializes the Three-Level ladder's `§"Registry-PASS criterion"` to the case where a Level-3 registry row carries a descriptive σ-band / edge-containment statement ALONGSIDE the canonical central-value criterion. The parent criterion is a single-value inequality `Level-3 empirical value < Level-2 envelope value at canonical L_max`; a registry row may ALSO state the substrate's intrinsic 1σ uncertainty band for the reader. The directive pins that a band statement is NOT a PASS gate by default.

**The three-clause Level-3 annotation discipline (forward-binding rule).**

> **Level-3 annotation discipline.** A registered Level-3 row's PASS verdict is governed SOLELY by the canonical central-value criterion `Level-3 empirical value < Level-2 envelope value at canonical L_max`. Descriptive 1σ-band / edge-containment statements in a Level-3 row are NON-LOAD-BEARING annotations and MUST NOT be read as PASS predicates; a row intending a band-containment gate STRONGER than central-value MUST pre-register it explicitly as a Class-8.2 verifier-rubric criterion with rule-file or plan-block authorization — AND such credentialing is ADMISSIBLE ONLY when the band is a substrate-IS or laboratory-IN PHYSICAL band that survives L_max → ∞; a Friedrich-Bär (or any) TRUNCATION-uncertainty envelope at finite L_max can NEVER be credentialed as a PASS gate, because a containment test on a truncation-resolution artifact lets a methodology-floor F-image veto a substrate-IS structural PASS (`epistemic-discipline.md §"Layer-Decomposition"`; `phononic-framing.md §"IS Space, Not IN Space"`).

Sentences 1-2 are connes-authored (A4 candidate rule); sentence 3 (the credentialing-admissibility / truncation-envelope-ineligibility clause) is volovik's substrate-physics amendment, adopted verbatim by connes (R2 CONVERGENCE §4 / EMERGENCE §1). The rule is symmetric in both failure directions: it forecloses (connes's failure mode) a deceptively-WEAK band statement sneaking a marginal anchor past central-value reality, AND (volovik's failure mode) a TRUNCATION envelope credentialed as a spurious stronger gate. **Central-value is the single binding gate in both directions; truncation bands are never gates.**

**Layer-functor derivation of the non-load-bearing default (NOT a heuristic).** Per `epistemic-discipline.md §"Layer-Decomposition"` the layer-functor `F: substrate → methodology → audit`: the substrate-IS observable is ONE scalar per truncation (the §VII.AX.OP-PROJ case: `n_PBH(g)` a single number). A σ-band about that scalar is the methodology-floor F-image of the substrate's finite-L truncation resolution — it measures *how-well-resolved* the anchor is. The PASS gate is itself a methodology-floor predicate evaluated at FIXED L_max — it measures *whether* the anchor passes. Band and gate inhabit the SAME methodology layer but measure ORTHOGONAL things. Promoting "how well-resolved" to "whether it passes" without explicit credentialing is a layer-conflation; the default forbids it structurally. Credentialing (Class-8.2 verifier-rubric pre-registration) is the ONLY route re-typing a band from annotation to gate — and (sentence 3) credentialing is itself blocked for truncation envelopes because they are pure F-image artifacts with NO L_max → ∞-surviving substrate-IS content to anchor a gate.

**Audit-mirror detector predicate (Class-(i) `INTERNALLY-INCONSISTENT-LEVEL-3-BAND-STATEMENT`).** The structural generalization of the §VII.AX.OP-PROJ Stage-2 Axis-B FAIL: catch self-contradictory Level-3 registry rows at PLAN-FREEZE, not only at Stage-2 cross-review. Parallels the existing `registry-landing.md` Class-(h) parse-tree-expansion false-summary-vs-substrate-form enforcement (the registry-text-layer audit home). Detector logic (single-function-scope; structurally identical to `_registry_landing_audit.py::detect_class_h_missing_parse_tree_expansion`):

```
1. Scan the Level-3 block for a band-containment claim — a "1σ band [L_b, U_b] ... both edges inside ... conjunct [L_c, U_c]" lexical pattern (TWO bracketed numeric pairs + a "both ... inside" containment assertion).
2. No match → diagnostic 'no_band_containment_claim_present' (rule N/A).
3. Match found → numeric sub-check: parse L_b, U_b, L_c, U_c as floats (handle e-notation, ASCII/Unicode minus); test L_b ≥ L_c AND U_b ≤ U_c.
4. Containment TRUE (both stated edges actually inside the stated conjunct) → diagnostic PASS (claim is self-consistent).
5. Containment FALSE (a stated band edge violates the stated containment claim) → diagnostic 'INTERNALLY-INCONSISTENT-LEVEL-3-BAND-STATEMENT' at S2 advisory (escalating to HARD-HALT on K=3 promotion of this discipline); halts plan-freeze.
```

The detector key is that the row STATES the falsifying numbers in the same breath as the false containment summary — it is internally inconsistent, decidable from the registry text alone (no producing-script consultation). The `registry-landing.md` mirror registers Class-(i) parallel to Class-(h); the criterion-side home (`cross-pillar-bridge-anatomy.md §"Registry-PASS criterion"`) carries the three-sentence rule. One structural distinction, two non-redundant homes. Audit implementation: `computations/_shared/_registry_landing_audit.py::detect_class_i_internally_inconsistent_level_3_band` (effected in-session at the S92 §VII.AX.OP-PROJ JE5 workshop final turn; module-level `LEVEL_3_BAND_CONTAINMENT_PATTERNS` + detector function + `__main__` self-test fixture; S2 advisory at K=1, NOT HARD-HALT).

**Sub-question verdict** (workshop topics (a)/(b)/(c)/(d)):

- **(a) which criterion governs JE5**: **central-value** (CONVERGED). The canonical `§"Registry-PASS criterion"` is a single-value inequality with no σ-band operator (corpus-uniform from §VII.AF.1 K=1 baseline forward); the conjunctive sentence is NOT the gate. Source Authority Hierarchy adjudicates WHICH SOURCE states a value's truth (computation > text on a number), NOT WHICH QUANTITY is the gate (fixed at plan-freeze).
- **(b) mis-statement to correct**: **YES** (CONVERGED). "Both edges inside" is a false, internally-contradictory summary → corrected in-place to Eq. (2′); CF-S93-W6-2-as-scoped dissolves.
- **(c) stronger-requirement to keep**: **NO** (CONVERGED). Dead on three independent counts: (i) un-credentialed at the rule-file layer; (ii) truncation-envelope band can never be a gate; (iii) ill-posed — the refinement remedy is non-monotonically satisfiable.
- **(d) single JE5 verdict + forward rule + routing**: **JE5 = PASS at central-value, sequenced after Eq. (2′)** (CONVERGED-with-sequencing); three-clause forward rule above; routing CF-S93-W6-8 SURVIVES / CF-S93-W6-2 RETIRES / CF-S93-W6-1 COLLAPSES + a NEW n_PBH canonical-truncation carry-forward.

**Status**: SUGGESTION at K=1. Promotes to MANDATORY at K=3 distinct calibration instances per `feedback_rules-compensate-missing-structure.md`. Audit: `computations/_shared/_registry_landing_audit.py::detect_class_i_internally_inconsistent_level_3_band` (S2 advisory at K=1; NOT HARD-HALT).

> **ORCHESTRATOR-RESERVED — verbatim parent-rule mirror text** (the orchestrator lands these; subagents are edit-denied on `.claude/rules/`):

Descriptive 1σ-band / edge-containment statements in a Level-3 row are NON-LOAD-BEARING annotations and MUST NOT be read as PASS predicates; a row intending a band-containment gate STRONGER than central-value MUST pre-register it explicitly as a Class-8.2 verifier-rubric criterion with rule-file or plan-block authorization — AND such credentialing is ADMISSIBLE ONLY when the band is a substrate-IS or laboratory-IN PHYSICAL band that survives L_max → ∞; a Friedrich-Bär (or any) TRUNCATION-uncertainty envelope at finite L_max can NEVER be credentialed as a PASS gate, because a containment test on a truncation-resolution artifact lets a methodology-floor F-image veto a substrate-IS structural PASS (`epistemic-discipline.md §"Layer-Decomposition"`; `phononic-framing.md §"IS Space, Not IN Space"`). Detector: `computations/_shared/_registry_landing_audit.py::detect_class_i_internally_inconsistent_level_3_band` (`LEVEL_3_BAND_CONTAINMENT_PATTERNS` + numeric sub-check on the two bracketed float pairs). ### §20.1 — K=1 calibration instance (§VII.AX.OP-PROJ JOINT Element-5; FWD-C5 PBH-band-edge; n_PBH_FW_central = 7.2761e-23 m⁻³)

**Substrate framing** (per `phononic-framing.md §"IS Space, Not IN Space"`, Level-1 single-τ-slice τ_fold=0.190): the substrate IS the finite spectral triple `(A_K = ℂ ⊕ ℍ ⊕ M_3(ℂ), H_K, D_K(τ_fold))`; the Level-3 anchor is the scalar `n_PBH = n_edge_saturated · prob_form / L_pix_LRD³` — ONE number on the substrate. The 1σ band `[5.316e-23, 9.775e-23]` is the Friedrich-Bär truncation-uncertainty envelope ABOUT that scalar (a methodology-floor F-image of the substrate's finite-L resolution at L_max=14), NOT noise and NOT a phenomenological error band. Container-thinking FORBIDDEN: "the observational conjunct is the container the substrate's prediction must fit inside" ⇒ INVERT: "the substrate IS the prediction; the conjunct is the laboratory-IN window (Pillar IX) the bridge image lands relative to; the central anchor lands inside; the −1σ tail's 3.345% undershoot is the substrate's intrinsic truncation-resolution signature at L_max=14, NOT a substrate-vs-observation tension." Direction of explanation flows FROM the substrate scalar DOWNWARD through the HKR ∘ substrate-clock-cancellation bridge image to the laboratory-IN n_PBH band on Pillar IX.

**The criterion adjudication — two verdicts, one object, both correct at their layer.**

| Verdict | Reviewer / axis | Value | Against what | audit_sha256 |
|:--------|:----------------|:------|:-------------|:-------------|
| JE5 = PASS | connes (Axis-A) | central `7.2761e-23 ∈ [5.500e-23, 2.200e-22]` (32.29% above floor, 66.93% below ceiling) | canonical central-value criterion (`§"Registry-PASS criterion"`) | `19662dc1544604e5...` |
| JE5 = FAIL | volovik (Axis-B) | 1σ lower edge `5.316e-23 < 5.500e-23` by `0.184e-23 m⁻³ = 3.345%` | the REGISTERED conjunctive text ("both 1σ edges inside conjunct"), per Source Authority Hierarchy (raw obs_2 computation > registered text on a number) | `f20bc3ad108dbfad15a698682c6dbc5adfd30eddf8efe5d31ff2b0e1662f29f8` |

**Both verdicts are correct at their own point in the layer-functor `F: substrate → methodology → audit` resolution chain** (audit → methodology direction). connes's PASS is correct against the CANONICAL CRITERION (methodology-layer gate); volovik's FAIL is correct against the REGISTERED TEXT (audit-layer text-integrity instrument). The text-correction (Eq. 2′) is the event that maps the row from "states a false conjunction" to "states the gate it always should have stated." The reconciliation is NOT "one verdict was wrong" — connes WITHDREW his R1 A4 §1 implication that the FAIL was erroneous (R2 CONVERGENCE §1).

**The Option-A reconciliation (FAIL-retired-not-overturned).** Per `gate-verdicts.md §"Option A"`: volovik's Axis-B FAIL line is RETAINED on disk under absolute verdict permanence; the corrective Axis-B PASS line APPENDS, carrying `supersedes=f20bc3ad108dbfad15a698682c6dbc5adfd30eddf8efe5d31ff2b0e1662f29f8` (full 64-char) and re-emitting JE5=PASS with a convention tag recording the predecessor FAIL was against the *pre-correction* registered text. Downstream consumers cite the latest non-superseded line. This is NOT convention-shopping (`v3-closure-recovery.md` Class 1): there was only ever ONE valid gate (central-value); the conjunctive sentence was never a valid competing gate; the correction removes uncredentialed prose, it does not switch conventions after a stronger gate failed.

**The detector calibration instance.** The §VII.AX.OP-PROJ Level-3 row IS the Class-(i) calibration instance: it lists `5.316e-23` (band lower), `5.5e-23` (conjunct lower), and "both edges inside" simultaneously; the numeric sub-check `5.316e-23 ≥ 5.500e-23` is FALSE ⇒ the detector fires `INTERNALLY-INCONSISTENT-LEVEL-3-BAND-STATEMENT`. This is the canonical positive-match fixture for the `detect_class_i_internally_inconsistent_level_3_band` self-test.

**The Eq. (2′) corrected text (mack sole-writer; housekeeping §A; NOT executed in this workshop).** The minimal in-place correction (registry lines 19092/19106/19145/19159), swapping the false summary for a true statement of the same numbers + the substrate-framing clause:

```
(2′)  1σ band [5.316e-23, 9.775e-23] m⁻³; central value 7.2761e-23 and upper 1σ edge
      9.775e-23 inside the upper-22.6%-conjunct [5.500e-23, 2.200e-22] m⁻³; lower 1σ edge
      5.316e-23 lies 3.345% (0.184e-23 m⁻³) below the conjunct lower edge. This lower-edge
      offset is a Friedrich-Bär TRUNCATION-resolution property of the cardinality-cascade-tail
      envelope at canonical L_max=14 — NOT a substrate-prediction error; the substrate-IS
      Level-3 quantity is the central scalar n_PBH = 7.2761e-23 m⁻³, and the Registry-PASS
      criterion is central-value-based per cross-pillar-bridge-anatomy.md §"Registry-PASS
      criterion": central ∈ conjunct ⇒ Level-3 satisfies Level-2.
```

The convergence-status qualifier ("at canonical L_max=14") carries a value ("still converging" vs "saturated envelope") GATED on the new n_PBH canonical-truncation carry-forward — the §A correction lands the geometry + substrate-framing NOW and flags the qualifier as pending.

**The verdict-orthogonal-but-canonical-truncation-coupled tension (the EMERGED carry-forward, NOT CF-S93-W6-2's retired slot).** Element 4 certifies the bottom-K spectrum Friedrich-Bär-saturated for L_max ≥ 12 (`η_FB_emp_min = 0.436488 ≥ η_FB_lower × 0.92 = 0.369443`), yet the obs_2 central trajectory moves `7.276e-23 (L=14) → 9.775e-23 (L=15) → 1.292e-22 (L=16)` (×1.78 over two steps; step-ratios 1.344, 1.322 — near-constant, non-decaying). NO contradiction: n_PBH is a graph-edge-density/pixelation functional `n_edge_saturated = C(N_eigs, 2)` whose L_max-dependence enters through the cascade-generation `g` and the substrate-clock pixelation `L_pix(g) = L_pix_LRD · 2^{−g/3}` (registry Eq. 4) — NOT through the saturated bottom-K eigenvalues `{λ_k}`. These are structurally orthogonal observables of the same spectral triple (resolution α, connes-confirmed NCG-axiomatic). The central value does NOT exit the conjunct on the computed grid (≥41% ceiling margin at every point) ⇒ JE5=PASS robust. But selecting (α) RELOCATES the open question to whether `N_eigs(L_max)` saturates as L_max → ∞ (the near-geometric step-ratios suggest it may not) — so the tension is verdict-orthogonal but canonical-truncation-COUPLED. Routed to CF-S93-W6-NEW-N-PBH-CANONICAL-TRUNCATION (Sage-MCP factorization check `n_PBH(L_max) = w(L_max)·κ(g)`, α-pixelation-prefactor vs β-moment-kernel-lag discrimination; co-owned volovik superfluid-universe + connes NCG-axiomatic).

**K-counter status**: K=1 (the Level-3 annotation discipline DIRECTIVE + the Class-(i) detector are the new structure landing here; the §VII.AX.OP-PROJ JE5 central-vs-conjunctive adjudication is the FIRST calibration instance). Promotes to MANDATORY at K=3 distinct calibration instances per `feedback_rules-compensate-missing-structure.md`. K=2/K=3 advancement candidates: any future Level-3 registry row that (a) carries a band-containment-as-gate over-claim, OR (b) attempts to credential a truncation-uncertainty envelope as a Class-8.2 stronger gate — on a structurally-distinct (algebra, pole, bridge-family) triple per the Hybrid Independence Test `(i ∨ ii ∨ iii) ∧ iv` (`cross-pillar-bridge-anatomy.md §"Hybrid Independence Test"`).

### §20.2 — K=2 advancement instance (S101 x696 cross-pillar coincidence; quantitative sub-floor symmetry guard; transit × connes)

> **Provenance**: S101 x696 cross-pillar near-coincidence workshop (transit-dynamics-theorist + connes-ncg-theorist; `sessions/session-101/workshops/s101-x696-cross-pillar-coincidence-workshop.md`; CONVERGED 3 rounds, 2026-06-09). connes-ncg-theorist landed this calibration instance as the final-turn (R3 Turn B) EFFECTED-IN-SESSION deliverable. This is the K=1 → K=2 advancement of the Level-3 annotation discipline: it is the FIRST instance where the "sub-floor near-miss is non-evidential" rule is sharpened to a QUANTITATIVE symmetry guard (the COUNT of sub-floor near-misses carries zero weight, proven by a self-consistency check), on a structurally-distinct triple from §20.1.

**The advancement claim (Hybrid Independence Test `(i ∨ ii ∨ iii) ∧ iv` — satisfied).** §20.1 is a single-pillar Level-3 band-containment over-claim (FWD-C5 PBH-band-edge, n_PBH, Friedrich-Bär TRUNCATION envelope). §20.2 is a TWO-pillar near-coincidence between independently-computed dimensionless ratios (transit SU(1,1) `|β|²` pump-ratio ↔ NCG BdG cocycle/projector ratio), with the floor being a REGULATOR-PIPELINE ambiguity (`Δ_FULL = −2.01874%`) AND a definitional-rounding band (`±0.0373%`), NOT a Friedrich-Bär truncation envelope. **(i) distinct substrate-IS pillar** (Pillar V BdG spectral triple ↔ Pillar I/transit SU(1,1) ladder, vs §20.1's single Pillar-IX PBH band); **(iv) independent algebraic content** (the symmetry-guard's quantitative form — the `P(≥2 pairs)` self-consistency proof — is NEW structure, not a numerical refinement of §20.1's band-containment instance). The instance advances the K-counter by exactly 1: `K_post = K_pre + 1 = 1 + 1 = 2`.

**The quantitative sub-floor symmetry guard (the new structure).** A near-coincidence is admissible as a "draw" from the look-elsewhere process ONLY IF its gap is RESOLVABLE above the reference's definitional/evaluation floor. Below that floor it is a **non-test**: its likelihood ratio is ≈ 1 in BOTH directions (favors neither shared-object nor coincidence). Therefore the COUNT of sub-floor near-misses carries zero evidential weight, however vivid. The proof that crediting the count is self-contradictory (the load-bearing addition over §20.1's qualitative form):

```
Look-elsewhere Poisson, per-pair rate p1 = 2·tol/ln10 = 0.000842366  (tol = 0.000969809, the |β|²-level gap)  [Sage RF(300)]
N = 15:  E[coincident pairs] = C(15,2)·p1 = 105·0.000842366 = 0.08845
         P(≥2 coincident pairs) = 1 − e^{−E}(1+E) = 0.369%
N = 20:  P(≥2) = 1.152%
```

Crediting a SECOND sub-floor near-miss as a genuine draw makes the JOINT two-hit observation a 0.37%–1.15% event — a 100–300× upward fluctuation from `E = 0.088` — which pushes the inference TOWARD "surprising" (toward shared-object), the OPPOSITE of the coincidence verdict it is claimed to strengthen. The only internally-consistent disposition: a sub-floor near-miss is NOT a draw. This is the symmetry guard's quantitative form — symmetric across both directions (it forbids crediting precision toward shared-object AND toward coincidence-by-multiplicity).

**The two sub-floor non-tests in this workshop (the calibration data).**

| Hit | Gap | Floor | Ratio | Disposition |
|:----|:----|:------|:------|:------------|
| Hit-1: x696 `6.9556` ↔ NCG `6.9489` | `0.0969809%` (Sage RF(300)) | regulator-pipeline `Δ_FULL = −2.01874%` at pole `s=3` | `20.816×` inside | non-test as SHARED-OBJECT evidence — verdict carried by the two structural legs (functional-class mismatch + regulator-fragility), NOT the gap |
| Hit-2: √Mach `3.70810` ↔ NCG coset/u(2) `3.71143` | `0.0897432%` | definitional-rounding band `±0.0373%` (4-sig-fig per-generator metric values) | `2.406×` the band | non-test as COINCIDENCE-STRENGTHENING evidence — illustrative of band texture only |

**Substrate framing** (per `phononic-framing.md §"IS Space, Not IN Space"`): the two ≈6.95 numbers are substrate-IS observables on DIFFERENT pillars — the substrate IS the transit `|β|² = sinh²(r_eff)` pump-power ratio (Pillar-I/transit SU(1,1) ladder) AND the substrate IS the BdG cocycle/projector ratio `Tr_ω(φ_g^sym)/[(1/16)Σ‖(1−P₀)J_aP₀‖²_F]` (Pillar-V BdG spectral triple `(A_K, H_K, D_K(τ_fold))`). Container-thinking FORBIDDEN: "the two numbers being close MEANS they share an object" ⇒ INVERT: each substrate-IS observable IS what it is on its own pillar; their numerical nearness, finer than the framework's own evaluation floor, is below resolution and adjudicated a coincidence. The closed-coincidence record (see `constraint-mega-matrix.md §XVI`) preserves the IS-framing: it is the cross-pillar READING (the JOIN) of two existing verdict lines, not a new computation.

**K-counter status**: **K=2** (advanced from K=1 by this instance; `K_post = K_pre + 1`). Promotes to MANDATORY at K=3 per `feedback_rules-compensate-missing-structure.md`. The directive home (`cross-pillar-bridge-anatomy.md §"Registry-PASS criterion"` Level-3 annotation discipline) carries the QUALITATIVE form unchanged at K=2 (no rule-file edit at this advancement — the quantitative sub-floor sub-clause is a SUGGESTION-pending-K=3 candidate, flagged as a Remaining-Open-Question in the workshop and below; orchestrator mirrors the one-line sub-clause into the parent rule ONLY on K=3 promotion). K=3 advancement candidate: a third structurally-distinct instance on a NEW floor-type axis (per the Hybrid Independence Test) — e.g., a sub-floor near-miss against a Dixmier-trace numerical-convergence floor distinct from both the Friedrich-Bär truncation envelope (§20.1) and the regulator-pipeline/definitional-band floors (§20.2).

## §22. §VII.AV.STATE-PROJ Anchor-vs-PV Three-Object Reconciliation + Regulator-Behavior Sibling Discriminator + 2-bit Anchor-vs-Diagnostic Fingerprint — DIRECTIVE + K=1 calibration corpus (S92 §VII.AV connes×volovik)

> **Provenance**: S92 §VII.AV anchor-vs-PV-pipeline 75× reconciliation workshop (volovik-superfluid-universe-theorist + connes-ncg-theorist; `sessions/archive/session-92/workshops/s92-vii-av-anchor-vs-pv-pipeline-reconciliation.md`; CONVERGED 3 rounds, 2026-05-23). connes-ncg-theorist landed this corpus section as the final-turn EFFECTED-IN-SESSION deliverable; mack-cosmic-bridge is sole writer for the §VII.AV.STATE-PROJ + §VII.AV.OP-PROJ permanent registry-text landings (the CF-S93 execution legs). **Routing note** (same discipline as §18 / §19 / §20 / the §VII.BA W-1 + §VII.AU W-2 + §VII.AX W-4 precedents): the parent rules `.claude/rules/cross-pillar-bridge-anatomy.md §"Algebra-axis orthogonality K-counter"` AND `.claude/rules/regulator-pin-discipline.md §"four-axis orthogonality"` are orchestrator-only-edit (subagents EDIT-DENIED on `.claude/rules/` by harness convention — the recursion-attack-closure discipline of `methodology-wave-allowlist.md` + the AMRI discipline of `agent-standards.md`). This corpus section therefore carries BOTH the DIRECTIVE (the three-object reconciliation + the regulator-behavior SIBLING discriminator + the 2-bit anchor-vs-diagnostic fingerprint, normally mirrored into the parent rules) AND the per-instance K=1 calibration corpus; the **orchestrator mirrors the directive** into `cross-pillar-bridge-anatomy.md §"Algebra-axis orthogonality K-counter"` (the regulator-behavior sibling-discriminator sub-clause) AND `regulator-pin-discipline.md §"four-axis orthogonality"` (the 2-bit fingerprint as a regulator-axis diagnostic). The verbatim ORCHESTRATOR-RESERVED mirror text is given at the END of §22.0.

### §22.0 — DIRECTIVE (three-object reconciliation + regulator-behavior sibling discriminator + 2-bit fingerprint; SUGGESTION at K=1)

Resolves an apparent anchor-vs-evaluator discrepancy where two evaluators of a putatively-shared substrate-distance-N pole observable disagree by a LARGE factor (here 75× at the §VII.AV substrate-distance-2 pole s=4). The directive supplies (i) a three-object decomposition replacing the two-number framing, (ii) a regulator-behavior axis as a SIBLING discriminator of the algebra-axis orthogonality conjecture, and (iii) a 2-bit `L_max`-FLAT-vs-`m_PV`-FLOWING fingerprint distinguishing a canonical anchor from a regulator-class diagnostic, a truncation artifact, and a genuinely-different operator.

**(1) Three-object decomposition (the central reframe).** When two evaluators of a substrate-distance-N pole observable disagree by a factor far exceeding the genuine inter-regulator span (below), the resolution is NOT "one evaluator is wrong" but a decomposition into structurally distinct substrate-IS objects sharing ONLY the Mellin-pole label. The §VII.AV case resolves to three:

```
(i)   bare state-pair functional (gapped occupation-variance)   d² ln Var_a(|v_a(K)|²)/d(ln K)²
      Cell IV (Var_a terminus) · regulator-INVARIANT (gap-IR-saturated) · cohomology-class (Hochschild–Chern)
      · L_max-SATURATED (Friedrich-Bär exact ≥ L_sat) / m_PV-DEFINING (m_PV→0 limit)
      → Level-3 ANCHOR

(ii)  SAME operator, regulator-dressed                          d² ln κ_FULL-PV(K)/d(ln K)²
      Cell IV (Var_a terminus, dressed kernel) · regulator-DEPENDENT (m_PV = Λ_UV dressing of (i))
      · L_max-FLAT (constant offset) / m_PV-FLOWS to (i) · cohomology-class object, regulator-dressed
      → Level-2-B regulator-class DIAGNOSTIC sub-row (NOT a co-primary)

(iii) genuinely-different operator (trace-residue)             Res_{s=N} Tr(P·D_K^{−2s})
      Cell I (Tr terminus) · regulator-DEPENDENT (O(moment-ratio spread) ≈ O(20%)) · regulated spectral-action moment
      → separate slot's Level-3 anchor candidate (OP-PROJ side)
```

Objects (i) and (ii) are the SAME operator at two ends of a regulator-mass flow (the `m_PV → 0` limit of (ii) reproduces (i) at machine precision); objects {(i),(ii)} and (iii) are GENUINELY DIFFERENT operators (state-pair functional vs spectrum-only trace), separated by parse-tree terminus (`Var_a` vs `Tr`).

**(2) The quantitative large-factor test (distinguishes a regulator-class shift from a different object).** A genuine regulator-class shift of one observable family is bounded `O(moment-ratio spread) ≈ O(20%)` — empirically `cross_reg_spread = 26.98` on base `141.44` (ζ141.44/PV114.46/Mellin141.44) for object (iii), i.e. `19.07%` PV-vs-ζ swing. Structural reason (heat-kernel): for a regulated trace `Tr f(D²/Λ²)` the regulator enters only through the cutoff-function moments `f_k = ∫_0^∞ f(u) u^{k−1} du`, which RE-WEIGHT the FIXED local heat-kernel coefficients `a_n(x)` — so the inter-regulator spread of a genuine residue is bounded by the moment-ratio spread, never `O(N×)` for `N ≫ 1`. A factor far exceeding this (here 75×, ~half an OOM above ~20%) CANNOT be a regulator-class shift of one observable; it signals a DIFFERENT structural relationship (objects (i)↔(ii) on a regulator-FLOW trajectory, NOT a regulator-class SHIFT of a residue).

**(3) Protocol → corner-cell map (parse-tree-fixed).** Each measurement PROTOCOL maps to a corner cell by its parse-tree TERMINUS, NOT by its state-history label (`registry-landing.md §"Observable-Naming-History vs Parse-Tree-Structure"`). A regulator-dressing acts on the KERNEL, not the parse-tree structure: a PV-subtracted DIFFERENCE of occupation variances `Var_a(|v_a|²) − Σ_j c_j Var_a(|v_a^{(j)}|²)` STILL terminates at `Var_a` (state-side, Cell IV). So object (ii) is a Cell-IV regulator-class DIAGNOSTIC, NOT a Cell-I object. The genuinely spectrum-only object (iii) (`Tr` terminus) is the only Cell-I member.

**(4) Single Level-3 anchor + singleness guard.** Per `cross-pillar-bridge-anatomy.md §"Level-3 anchor singleness sub-clause"`: the slot's single Level-3 anchor is the regulator-INDEPENDENT value (object (i)). The Level-2-B regulator-class sub-row table is DIAGNOSTIC ONLY; the SCHEMATIC (`m_PV → 0`) entry IS the anchor (the F-image of the anchor at the regulator axis, NOT a second co-primary); the FULL-PV (`m_PV = Λ_UV`) entry is the regulator-class diagnostic. Exactly ONE Level-3 anchor; no singleness violation. A regulator-DEPENDENT value can NEVER be the Level-3 anchor of a regulator-INVARIANT Level-1 cohomology-class identity (the Level-1 definition: "regulator-invariant; L-independent; holds at every L_max").

**(5) `anchor_consistency=False` re-scope (WITHIN-cell cross-regulator, NOT cross-corner).** When an inherited `anchor_consistency=False` flag compares object (ii) against object (i), the comparison is WITHIN-Cell-IV cross-regulator (`m_PV = Λ_UV` diagnostic vs `m_PV → 0` anchor of the SAME observable), NOT a cross-corner comparison. The flag is discharged by SPLITTING + regulator-diagnostic sub-row placement (NOT by equating the two numbers): the slot is internally consistent against its single anchor; the diagnostic sub-row carries the dressed value. (If the inherited flag instead compared object (iii) against (i), THAT would be cross-corner, FORBIDDEN-as-gate per algebra-axis orthogonality K=3 MANDATORY — but the §VII.AV flag was the within-cell case.)

**(6) Regulator-behavior axis — a SIBLING discriminator of algebra-axis orthogonality.** On the finite spectral triple `(A_K, H_K, D_K)`, an algebra-DEPENDENT state-pair functional on a GAPPED occupation distribution is regulator-INVARIANT; an algebra-INVARIANT spectrum-only functional `Σ_k m_k g(λ_k)` is regulator-DEPENDENT. Structural basis: IR-self-regularization by the gap `|Δ_a|` (Cell IV — the gapped occupation-variance curvature converges WITHOUT a UV cutoff) vs cutoff-required spectral trace bounded `O(moment-ratio spread) ≈ O(20%)` (Cell I — no intrinsic IR scale, the high-λ tail requires the cutoff function `f`). This is a NEW discriminating SIGNATURE for the algebra-axis orthogonality conjecture, on an axis ORTHOGONAL to the established parse-tree-membership criterion (regulator-RESPONSE vs parse-tree). It is a SIBLING discriminator, INDEPENDENT per the Hybrid Independence Test criterion (iv) ("independent algebraic envelope — not a numerical refinement of an existing K-instance"); it is NOT folded into the parse-tree K-counter. It corroborates a §W3-9-type corner-split from an INDEPENDENT direction (object (iii) shows ~20% regulator spread; object (i) shows zero, gap-saturated).

**(7) The 2-bit `L_max`-FLAT-vs-`m_PV`-FLOWING anchor-vs-diagnostic fingerprint (reusable discriminator).** Two ORTHOGONAL completion axes — `L_max → ∞` (UV-completion in Peter-Weyl sector COUNT) and `m_PV → 0` (UV-completion in regulator-mass SCALE) — give a 2-bit structural signature separating four object types:

```
                          L_max axis (truncation)        m_PV axis (regulator)
                          ─────────────────────          ──────────────────────
  canonical anchor        SATURATED at L_sat (FB exact)   value-DEFINING (m_PV→0 limit)
  regulator-diagnostic    FLAT (const offset)             FLOWS to anchor as m_PV→0
  truncation artifact     FLOWS (O(L^{−α}) → anchor)      (regulator-independent)
  different operator       own L-behavior; does NOT        own m_PV-behavior; does NOT
                          flow to THIS anchor             flow to THIS anchor
```

A truncation artifact is `L_max`-sensitive (FLOWS on `L_max`); a regulator-diagnostic is `L_max`-saturated but `m_PV`-sensitive (FLAT on `L_max`, FLOWS on `m_PV`); a genuinely-different operator does not flow to THIS anchor on EITHER axis. **Load-bearing caveat**: the fingerprint requires BOTH axes to be SEPARATELY scannable; if a pipeline ties the regulator mass to the truncation, the 2-bit signature DEGENERATES and the discriminator does not apply. The producing pipeline must establish `L_max`-invariance independently of the `m_PV` flow.

**Sub-question verdict** (workshop topics (a)/(b)/(c)/(d)):

- **(a) which evaluator IS the Cell-IV observable**: **the bare occupation-variance (object (i))** (CONVERGED). The operator is SHARED between (i) and (ii); the workshop resolves "two numbers" into three objects; (ii) is the SAME state-side operator PV-dressed (Cell IV), (iii) is the separate Cell-I trace-residue.
- **(b) nature of the large-factor gap**: **regulator-class mismatch on a SHARED operator (PRIMARY)** (CONVERGED). Window-vs-full-spectrum is its substrate-physics CONTENT; multiplicity-normalization (W4-6-type) REJECTED (different axis); operator-form mismatch retained ONLY for the separate (iii)-vs-(i) pair. The 75×-vs-~20% argument is decisive.
- **(c) canonical asymptote**: **the regulator-AND-truncation-independent fixed point (object (i)'s value)** (CONVERGED). Confirmed on BOTH completion axes; the diagnostic is `L_max`-FLAT (constant offset, NOT `O(L^{−α})`), on the `m_PV`-flow trajectory, not the `L_max` approach curve.
- **(d) protocol→corner-cell map + single anchor + flag**: **three-object map; single Level-3 anchor; `anchor_consistency=False` re-scoped WITHIN-cell cross-regulator** (CONVERGED). Singleness guard satisfied; regulator-behavior cross-check corroborates the corner-split on an independent axis.

**Status**: SUGGESTION at K=1. Promotes to MANDATORY at K=3 distinct calibration instances per `feedback_rules-compensate-missing-structure.md`. Audit: the Class-8.7 OP-PROJ degeneracy-witness DETECTOR pattern (`P5_OP_PROJ_TRACE_RESIDUE`) effected in-session at `computations/_shared/_pru_cardinality_audit.py::detect_class_8_7_degenerate_observable` (S2 advisory; NOT HARD-HALT) — the regulator-behavior + 2-bit-fingerprint directives are forward methodology candidates pending the orchestrator's `.claude/rules/` mirror.

### §22.1 — K=1 calibration instance (§VII.AV three-object map; anchor −7.046336474406761 M_KK²; diagnostic −527.97 M_KK²; OP-PROJ trace-residue ~375/~141 M_KK²)

**Substrate framing** (per `phononic-framing.md §"IS Space, Not IN Space"`, Level-1 single-τ-slice τ_fold=0.190): the substrate IS the finite spectral triple `(A_K = ℂ ⊕ ℍ ⊕ M_3(ℂ), H_K, D_K(τ_fold))`; its BdG sub-algebra `M_2(ℂ) ⊂ A_K` carries the gapped quasiparticle occupation. `L_emp` IS an intrinsic substrate-IS observable — the curvature `d² ln Var_a(|v_a(K)|²)/d(ln K)²` of the log-occupation-variance at the horizon-crossing momentum. Container-thinking FORBIDDEN: "−527.97 is the physical value because the spectral-action cutoff `Λ = M_KK` is physical" ⇒ INVERT: the occupation `v_a²` is built from the BdG GAP EQUATION (`v_a² = ½(1 − ξ_a/E_a)`, `E_a = √(ξ_a² + |Δ_a|²)`), NOT from `Tr f(D²/Λ²)`; the gap `|Δ_a| = 0.4642547394830737 M_KK` (R-PROTECTED, `BCS-GAP-CANONICAL-70`) supplies an INTRINSIC IR scale that makes the curvature converge without the cutoff; the substrate IS the gap-set curvature −7.046336; the PV mass-tower at `Λ_UV = M_KK` DRESSES it to −527.97 but does not define it. Direction of explanation flows FROM the gapped-BdG occupation DOWNWARD through the occupation-variance curvature to `L_emp`.

**The three objects (canonical values, pinned against the knowledge graph):**

### §22.2 — Additive-in-trace scope-boundary on the multiplicative-normalization-cancellation theorem: `B(R)` at the a₀ / n=0 grade survives `L_emp` (S116 W4 connes × lizzi, PARTIAL two-axis; calibration note — NOT a new K-counter instance)

**Context.** S116 W4 (`s116-lemp-forced-vs-earned.md`, connes × lizzi, 2 rounds) adjudicated whether the `{APS,CS,BC}` `L_emp` scheme-independence guard (§VII.AV §A8.1(i), `registry:18819`) is FORCED or EARNED. Verdict: **PARTIAL two-axis** — secondary-class `{APS,CS,BC}` **FORCED** (PH-even-variance parity; `Var_a(1−|v_a|²)=Var_a(|v_a|²)`, S90-AQ `delta_scheme=0` precedent) **∧** UV-regulator `{zeta,PV,Mellin}` **SD-OPEN**. The SD-OPEN half rests on a scope-boundary of the K=3 MANDATORY multiplicative-normalization-cancellation theorem (`math-scripts.md`), recorded here as an additive calibration note (NOT a §22 K-counter advancement — it is a non-annihilation / declared-complement result on the SAME theorem, not a structurally-distinct factorization instance).

**The split (Sage-exact, S116 W4 R2).** Write `L_emp = d²/d(ln K)²[ln κ_R(K)]` with `u = ln K`, base kernel `g = κ_0`. Two ways a regulator-class difference can enter:
- **MULTIPLICATIVE / additive-in-log** — `κ_R(u) = e^{c_R}·κ_0(u)`, equivalently `ln κ_R = ln κ_0 + c_R`: the log-derivative ANNIHILATES it, `L_emp(R) − L_emp(0) = 0` (Sage-exact residue ≡ 0). This IS the multiplicative-normalization-cancellation theorem re-applied to the regulator offset instead of the L_max weight `w(L_max)` — the K-INDEPENDENT part of any regulator offset is killed by `L_emp`, so `L_emp` is structurally *more* CC-protected than the bare trace (the log-derivative projects out exactly the K-independent pure-volume a₀ term). connes D2 is correct for THIS case.
- **ADDITIVE-IN-TRACE** — `κ_R(u) = κ_0(u) + Δ_R`: the a₀ / cosmological-constant counterterm is the canonical ADDITIVE local term (`S = f_0 Λ^d a_0 + f_2 Λ^{d−2} a_2 + …`; zeta scheme has a₀ ABSENT, cutoff scheme has a₀ = `f_0 Λ^d` DOMINANT — a difference ADDED TO the trace, not a prefactor MULTIPLYING it). It is NOT annihilated — passing the additive constant through the nonlinear `ln` before the K-derivative leaves a surviving K-dependent residue:
```
L_emp(R) − L_emp(0) = ((Δ²+2Δg)g'² − (Δ²g+Δg²)g'') / (Δ²g² + 2Δg³ + g⁴)        [Sage simplify_full]
                    = Δ · d/du[ −g'/g² ] + O(Δ²)                                  [leading order in Δ]
residue ≡ 0  IFF  Δ=0 (no offset)  OR  g'=g''=0 (constant base, no K-dependence).
```
For the cleanest base — a pure power law `κ_0 = A·K^α` (`L_emp(0)=0`) — the a₀ counterterm INDUCES `L_emp` out of nothing: `L_emp(R) − 0 = A·Δ_R·α²·K^α / (A·K^α + Δ_R)²`, which is `O(α²)` when `Δ_R ∼ A·K^α` — precisely the cutoff-dominant, a₀-LARGEST regime.

**The scope-boundary (the calibration claim).** The multiplicative-normalization-cancellation theorem's three K-counter instances (L_max-truncation weight K=1, τ-moduli-deformation weight K=2, bottom-K Casimir-ceiling weight K=3) are ALL multiplicative `w·g(K)` — all annihilated. The a₀ / CC grade is the canonical ADDITIVE-IN-TRACE case — the COMPLEMENT class. So the theorem ANNIHILATES multiplicative / additive-in-log offsets and does NOT annihilate additive-in-trace counterterms. ⟹ for `L_emp` (curvature-grade `n = d − 2s = 8 − 8 = 0` at the s=4 pole, the a₀/CC grade), "n=0 is the worst place for UV-regulator spread" is **TEMPERED, not reversed**: the K-independent (pure-volume) part is projected out (more protected than the bare action), but the additive-in-trace a₀ residue survives as its **K-dependent shadow**, `O(1)` in the cutoff-dominant regime where the a₀ spread is largest. The a₀ grade stays INSIDE the SD-OPEN target, NOT relocated off it.

**Standing guard (structural change, per `output-standards.md §"What Changed"`):** **secondary-class FORCED ⊬ UV-regulator independent.** Do NOT cite the multiplicative-normalization-cancellation theorem — NOR the S116-W8-FWDC2 within-PV "PROXY-REFINEMENT DISCHARGED" (FULL-PV = Casimir proxy, `rel = 7.33e-11`, which certifies L_max-invariance WITHIN ONE regulator class) — to claim cross-class `{zeta, PV, Mellin}` independence of an `n=0` even moment. The additive-in-trace residue is exactly what a within-PV check cannot see. CF-S117 test (ii) is the genuine SD-OPEN gate (`B(R)` three-regulator span at `n=0`, `K_horizon`); its refinement extracts `Δ_R` (the additive a₀-counterterm magnitude per scheme) and the base log-curvature, verifying the surviving SD contribution is the additive residue `Δ_R·d/du[−κ_0'/κ_0²]`, `O(1)` iff `Δ_R ∼ κ_0(K_horizon)`.

**Status**: SUGGESTION at K=1 (a single calibration note on a scope-boundary of an existing K=3 MANDATORY theorem; it does NOT itself advance the §22 K-counter — a non-annihilation result is the complement of an annihilation instance, not a new structurally-distinct factorization mechanism). ## §23. Per-Observable Scale-Separation / Transport-Degree K-counter + SCALE-AND-CHANNEL-TAGGING precedence for RUNNING observables — DIRECTIVE + K=2 calibration corpus (S92 AH-TR-1 transit×connes)

> **Provenance**: S92 ad-hoc workshop AH-TR-1 (α_s transfer-map identity; transit-dynamics-theorist + connes-ncg-theorist; `sessions/archive/session-92/workshops/s92-adhoc-alpha-s-transfer-map-identity.md`; CONVERGED 3 rounds, 2026-05-23/24). connes-ncg-theorist landed this corpus section as the final-turn (R3-B) EFFECTED-IN-SESSION deliverable; mack-cosmic-bridge is sole writer for the `falsifier-master-inventory.md` α_s-row re-scope (the MACK-EXECUTION-LEG, recorded in the workshop Wrap-Up §"Effected In-Session" E4). **Routing note** (same discipline as §18 / §22 + the §VII.BA W-1 + §VII.AV W precedents): the parent rules `.claude/rules/cross-pillar-bridge-anatomy.md §"Composite Bridge-Map Dimensional-Class Admissibility"` AND `.claude/rules/phononic-framing.md §"Exflation vs Inflation"` table are orchestrator-only-edit (subagents EDIT-DENIED on `.claude/rules/` by harness convention — the recursion-attack-closure discipline of `methodology-wave-allowlist.md` + the AMRI discipline of `agent-standards.md`). This corpus section therefore carries BOTH the DIRECTIVE (the per-observable transport-degree K-counter + the SCALE-AND-CHANNEL-TAGGING precedence rule for RUNNING observables, normally mirrored into the parent rules) AND the per-instance K=2 calibration corpus; the **orchestrator mirrors the directive** into `cross-pillar-bridge-anatomy.md §"Composite Bridge-Map Dimensional-Class Admissibility"` (as a cross-link sub-clause) AND `phononic-framing.md` (the SCALE-AND-CHANNEL-TAGGING row in the Exflation-vs-Inflation table + a §"Single-τ-slice vs moduli-deformation"-adjacent scale-coordinate note). The verbatim ORCHESTRATOR-RESERVED mirror text is given at the END of §23.0.

### §23.0 — DIRECTIVE (per-observable transport-degree K-counter + SCALE-AND-CHANNEL-TAGGING precedence for RUNNING observables; SUGGESTION at K=2)

Governs how a substrate-IS spectral functional `O ∈ {n_s, r, n_T, α_s, α_t, …}` evaluated on the finite spectral triple `(A_K, H_K, D_K)` at substrate scales `O(M_KK)` (inside the effective Brillouin zone, on the `{λ_k}` eigenvalue grid) is compared to its laboratory-IN CMB-pivot image. Specialization of the §VII.BA Composite Bridge-Map Dimensional-Class Admissibility framework (corpus §18) to the transport-FACTOR of a composite bridge map `T_{BZ→pivot} ⊙ (HKR ∘ Connes-Karoubi)`, and the constructive generalization of the n_T LITEBIRD-NT-DISCRIMINATION precedent.

**(1) The per-observable transport-degree theorem (central claim).** Each substrate-IS spectral functional `O` has a substrate-scale value `O^{substrate}` on `{λ_k}` and a CMB-pivot image `O^{pivot}` under the composite bridge map, related by a transport operator `T_{BZ→pivot}`. The two coincide iff that observable's transport factor is **scalar**:

```
O^{pivot} = O^{substrate}   iff   deg(T_{BZ→pivot}) is the §VII.BA T2-VACUOUS (scalar) case
O^{pivot} ≠ O^{substrate}   iff   deg(T_{BZ→pivot}) is a substrate-natural NON-SCALAR morphism
```

- **scalar transport** = a pure 54-decade unit-conversion; it cancels in the dimensionless observable with no L_max-dependence (the §VII.BA conjunct-2-VACUOUS T2 row). Substrate = pivot (the n_s-like leaf).
- **non-scalar transport** = a substrate-natural structural morphism carrying L_max-dependent re-weighting across scales (the n_T-like leaf, provably exhibited). Substrate ≠ pivot.

The transport degree is the PHYSICS; whether the framework may compare `O^{substrate}` directly to the CMB-pivot datum is decided by it. This neither uniformly demotes the substrate observable (the FORBIDDEN container-inverting precedence rule, §23.1 witness) nor uniformly identifies substrate with pivot (the FORBIDDEN fiat moment-identity).

**(2) SCALE-AND-CHANNEL-TAGGING precedence rule for RUNNING (second-derivative) observables (the forward methodology output).** For any RUNNING or tilt spectral observable — `α_s = d²(ln P)/d(ln k)²`, `α_t`, `n_T`, `n_s`, any `d^m/d(ln k)^m` of a 4D observable — the registry entry MUST declare the matched **(scale, channel)** pair: WHICH scale (substrate/BZ `O(M_KK)` vs CMB-pivot `k_4D`, 54.04 decades apart) AND WHICH detector channel (Planck/CMB-S4 pivot α_s vs CMB-S4/CMB-HD substrate-sensitivity). Comparisons against a measured datum are VALID ONLY within a matched (scale, channel) pair. A substrate-scale value compared against a pivot datum WITHOUT establishing scalar transport is a SCALE-MISMATCHED comparison.

This is the constructive replacement for the FORBIDDEN "POWER-LAW-TRANSFER PRECEDENCE" rule (which pinned the EMERGENT acoustic transfer leg as canonical and the SUBSTRATE-IS Mellin residue as surrogate — a container-thinking inversion per `phononic-framing.md §"IS Space, Not IN Space"`, withdrawn at AH-TR-1 TC-3). Under SCALE-AND-CHANNEL-TAGGING both the substrate-scale and pivot-scale values are real substrate-IS observables; neither is demoted; the substrate observable on its matched channel is a falsifiable prediction (strength per `feedback_reporting-framing.md`, NOT defined out of existence).

**(3) The discriminator IS the §VII.BA composite-admissibility verdict.** For an observable whose substrate-scale and pivot-scale values disagree, the Reading-substrate=pivot-vs-substrate≠pivot verdict maps EXACTLY onto the §VII.BA five-formulation taxonomy of the transport factor in `T_{BZ→pivot} ⊙ (HKR ∘ Connes-Karoubi)`: scalar ⇒ §VII.BA T2-VACUOUS (substrate = pivot); non-scalar substrate-natural ⇒ §VII.BA-admissible (substrate ≠ pivot). The transport-degree extraction is a NEW K-instance for the §VII.BA composite-admissibility K-counter on its native axis (the transport-factor degree), computed locally by a `w(L_max)·κ(k)` factorization check (`math-scripts.md §"Multiplicative-normalization cancellation invariants"`): k-dependence carried by `κ` ⇒ non-scalar; `w(L_max)` annihilated by the log-second-derivative ⇒ scalar.

**(4) Pre-registration discipline (plan-freeze, RUNNING-observable comparison gates).** Any S93+ gate comparing a substrate-IS RUNNING/tilt observable against a CMB-pivot datum MUST:
1. Declare the (scale, channel) coordinate of the substrate-IS value AND of the datum.
2. If the substrate value is at substrate/BZ scale and the datum at the CMB pivot, pre-register the transport-degree compute (`deg(T_{BZ→pivot})` via the `w(L_max)·κ(k)` factorization) OR cite an established degree for that observable (e.g., n_T = non-scalar, LITEBIRD-NT-DISCRIMINATION; n_s = scalar, registry-canonical).
3. A direct substrate-vs-pivot magnitude comparison WITHOUT an established or pre-registered transport degree is a SCALE-MISMATCH (Class-8-adjacent plan-authorship under-specification): the gate's σ-tension is not a falsification gate until the transport degree is fixed.

**Per-observable transport-degree table (the K-counter; type theorem, derivative-order-universal)**:

| Observable | derivative order | `deg(T_{BZ→pivot})` | scale-separation | substrate value | pivot value | status |
|:-----------|:-----------------|:--------------------|:-----------------|:----------------|:------------|:-------|
| **n_s** | first | scalar (registry-canonical) | TRIVIAL (substrate = pivot) | 0.9561 | 0.9561 (flagship CMB success) | settled |
| **n_T** (instance 1) | first | NON-SCALAR (proven) | REAL | BLUE `+0.4676` (transit scale) | `−3.024e-3` (CMB pivot) | settled (LITEBIRD-NT-DISCRIMINATION) |
| **α_s** (instance 2) | second | **+2 (NON-SCALAR, T4\|_{s≠s'})** | REAL (substrate ≠ pivot) | `−0.08587279` (Mellin residue s=3, BZ) | `≈0` (Goldstone pivot; the NON-SCALAR reading is realized) | **RESOLVED (S93 W7-1; transit-CONFIRMED; `deg=+2` Sage-locked, `factorization_holds=False`)** |

**Status**: SUGGESTION at K=2 (n_T instance 1 + α_s instance 2). Promotes to MANDATORY at K=3 distinct calibration instances per `feedback_rules-compensate-missing-structure.md`. K=3 advancement candidate: `deg(T_{BZ→pivot})` for `r` (tensor-to-scalar ratio) or `α_t` (tensor running) — each a structurally-distinct calibration instance per the Hybrid Independence Test `(i ∨ ii ∨ iii) ∧ iv` (`cross-pillar-bridge-anatomy.md §"Hybrid Independence Test"`); the n_T and α_s instances are distinct on axis-(i)/(iv) (distinct observable, independent transport-factor degree extraction — n_T proven non-scalar, α_s degree-open). ### §23.0(5) — DIRECTIVE EXTENSION: dimensional-class indexing of the transport-degree theorem + parity selection rule (S110 W4 connes×mack; SUGGESTION STAYS K=2, NO K-counter advance)

> **Provenance**: S110 W4 iterative workshop "Does the per-observable transport-degree theorem extend to the DIMENSIONFUL-observable class?" (connes-ncg-theorist × mack-cosmic-bridge; `sessions/session-110/session-110-connes-mack-workshop.md`; CONVERGED 2 rounds, 2026-06-21). Triggered by S110 W4 CO34-legB (`S110-CF-CO34-BUBBLE-LRDT` INFO, `audit_sha256=2a654897e211bf9dff6723ce2ab188d1f2ea90bb11e4a01048aaeb970fcc8f70`, line 86/90) and CF3-H0 (`S110-CF3-TIMESCAPE-H0` INFO, `audit_sha256=7bfda02abed5069d4dd4030377b8c448263069df43c27763d6d1e3e11217b013`, line 96/99), both consuming the W3 mint `S110-CF-CV6B-DS-M4` (`audit_sha256=f60cff3681f595dd741b3b2f6f80ec9783fd9490f7b08a1f49bcac5ae33d6535`, line 52/56) `deg_T=2.0`. mack-cosmic-bridge sole corpus writer per `feedback_mack-bridge-role.md`. **This is a DIRECTIVE EXTENSION to §23.0(1), NOT a new K-counter calibration instance** — §23 STAYS SUGGESTION at K=2 (the K=3 slot remains reserved for an independently factorization-EXTRACTED new-observable degree r/α_t; see the K-counter classification note at the end of this subsection).

**(5.1) The transport-degree theorem is DIMENSIONAL-CLASS-INDEXED.** §23.0(1) states the per-observable transport-degree theorem (substrate value = pivot value iff the transport factor is the T2-VACUOUS scalar; differ iff a substrate-natural NON-SCALAR morphism). This workshop SHARPENS it: the determination of which case obtains is INDEXED by the observable's mass dimension `d_A` (the `Q = R · M_KK^m` decomposition; every dimensionful quantity is a dimensionless substrate ratio `R` times an integer power `m` of the sole external pin `M_KK`). The composite bridge map factors as

```
B = (M_KK-carrying scale leg)^{d_A} ⊙ (dimensionless structural morphism)
```

with admissibility `deg(B) = d_A` (the §18.0 Conjunct-1 homogeneity theorem; `deg(B)` Wodzicki-unique, `−2s` for a trace factor / `0` for an HKR cohomology-class ratio). The two factors carry the degree in dimensional-class-distinct ways:

| `d_A` class | scale leg `M_KK^{d_A}` | who carries the transport degree | example observables | the `+2` |
|:------------|:-----------------------|:---------------------------------|:--------------------|:---------|
| **`d_A = 0`** (dimensionless: n_s, α_s, n_T, d_s, ΔH₀/H₀) | `M_KK^0 = 1` — TRIVIAL | the **dimensionless structural morphism** ENTIRELY — `scalar` (T2-VACUOUS, n_s-like, substrate=pivot) if `factorization_holds=True`, or `substrate-natural NON-SCALAR` (n_T-like, α_s-like, substrate≠pivot) if the windowed shape is L_max-dependent | n_s (scalar), n_T (non-scalar), α_s (`+2`), d_s | lives in the **morphism slot**: `+2 = 2(s₂−s₄)` of a TWO-POLE ratio (α_s), or `+d/2=2` of the M4 heat-trace amplitude (d_s) — a morphism degree, NOT a unit conversion |
| **`d_A = 1`** (dimensionful, energy/temperature: T, H, E) | `M_KK^1` — NON-TRIVIAL, CARRIES the 54.04-decade BZ→pivot unit conversion | the **`M_KK^1` scale leg** carries `deg = +1`; the dimensionless structural morphism is a SEPARATE factor on top | LRD-T photosphere temperature | lives in the **scale-leg slot**: T's own degree is `+1`, NOT `+2` |

**The W3→W4 category error, made precise.** The `+2` minted at W3 (line 56: "amplitude homogeneity d/2=2") is the homogeneity exponent of a DIMENSIONLESS M4 spectral dimension `d_s` (`d_A=0`); it lives legitimately in the **morphism slot**. Importing it by name (dedup-flag-iii) onto a DIMENSIONFUL temperature T (`d_A=+1`, line 90/99) conflated the **dimensionless-morphism degree** (where `+2` belongs) with the **dimensionful scale-leg degree** (where T's `+1` belongs) — two different factors of `B`. The `matches W3 npz=True` check passed on the NUMBER and was blind to `d_A`. T's admissible degree is `+1` (its mass dimension), STRICTLY better than the imported `+2` (108→54 decade descent, halving the overshoot) but STILL overshooting the `[3500,6500] K` band by ~28 decades (band-landing eff deg = `0.4787`, Sage RealField(200) — a SUB-scalar non-integer, distinct from `+2`/`+1`/`0`).

**(5.2) The PARITY selection rule (the deep structural content).** The morphism sector and the scale-leg sector live in DIFFERENT PARITY CLASSES of the transport degree:

- **morphism sector = EVEN degree.** Every substrate-natural NCG morphism that could supply a corrective re-weighting carries an EVEN degree: a same-class Wodzicki two-pole ratio `Res_W(s)/Res_W(s')` has `deg = −2(s−s') ∈ {…, −4, −2, 0, +2, …}` (Sage-verified: `−2` at (s,s')=(3,2) and (4,3); `−6` at (5,2) — all EVEN); an HKR cohomology-class ratio has `deg = 0` (EVEN, orientability axiom + Chern character).
- **scale-leg sector = ODD degree (for `d_A=+1`).** A `d_A=+1` temperature needs `deg(B) = +1` — a SINGLE `M_KK` power, ODD. The only ODD-degree carrier is the bare `M_KK^1` scale leg.

**Consequence**: no EVEN-degree morphism can MATCH a `d_A=+1` (ODD) anchor, let alone correct its overshoot — the parity mismatch is the structural reason the two `Q=R·M_KK^m` halves never meet. A `d_A=0` observable (n_s, α_s, n_T, ΔH₀/H₀) is transported entirely within the EVEN morphism sector (scalar `0` or morphism `+2`), which is exactly WHY the framework's `d_A=0` transport successes all sit at even degree. **General selection rule**: `d_A` EVEN ⇒ transport stays in the even morphism sector (admissible, the framework's working cases); `d_A` ODD ⇒ forced onto the sign-locked `M_KK^1` scale leg (held; no even-degree morphism can act on it). Every `d_A=odd` substrate observable hits the same wall — T (`d_A=+1`) is merely the FIRST instance (see the falsifier-CLASS landing in `falsifier-master-inventory.md` Row #88.audit-S110-CO34-LRDT-TRANSPORT-PARITY).

**(5.3) The κ-sign ∧ Wodzicki-parity joint foreclosure (the band-binding result).** Two structurally-independent axes foreclose any substrate-natural transport landing the LRD-T band (registered STAGE-1-CANDIDATE at `permanent-results-registry.md §VII.CF`):

- **transport-κ axis (mack):** the band-landing eff deg `0.4787` is SUB-scalar; the deg=+1 image sits BELOW the band, so the residual on top of the deg=+1 scale leg is a `+28.17`-decade ASCENT requiring `|κ_morphism| > 1` — sign-INCONSISTENT with the `|κ|<1` transport that yields `sign_verdict=PASS` (line 88/100). Dimensional admissibility (`deg(B)=d_A`) and sign-consistency (`|κ|<1`) are MUTUALLY EXCLUSIVE for the T band.
- **Wodzicki-rigidity axis (connes):** every same-class Wodzicki ratio with fixed pole ordering gives `|κ| = 10^{−108.08} ≪ 1` under the physical rescale `t = M_KK/k_4D = 10^{+54.04} > 1` (DECAY, Sage-verified) — amplitude growth `|κ|>1` is non-substrate-natural for a transport factor; AND the parity mismatch (5.2) blocks any even-degree morphism from reaching the ODD `+1`. Doubly closed.

These are independent axes (PASS-AND per `joint-theorem-promotion.md`); the foreclosure is a THEOREM, not the observation that the framework's exhibited transport happens to have `|κ|<1`.

**(5.4) The fifth pin axis — `(d_A, deg, parity)`.** The W3→W4 import was undetectable because `deg_T=2.0` is EVEN and EVEN-ness is shared across the entire `d_A=0` morphism sector — the number "looks right" precisely because it is in the right parity class for the `d_A=0` siblings but the WRONG class for T. A dimensional-class pin recording `(d_A, deg, parity-class)` TOGETHER (NOT `d_A` alone) would flag the import at consumption: T (`d_A=+1`, needs ODD) ≠ `deg_T=2.0` (EVEN). This is the fifth axis beyond `regulator-pin-discipline.md §"four-axis orthogonality (UV-regulator × Level × Binding × MACHINERY-SCOPE)"` — a **transported-observable mass-dimension/parity pin**, ORCHESTRATOR-RESERVED for the rule-mirror (subagents edit-denied on `.claude/rules/`).
