## §24. Fair-Comparison-Observable Discipline for Spectral-Dimension / Diffusion-Window Comparisons — DIRECTIVE + K=1 calibration corpus (S92 AH-PF-1 kk×landau)

> **Provenance**: S92 ad-hoc workshop AH-PF-1 (spectral-dimension d_s flow vs CDT; kaluza-klein-theorist + landau-condensed-matter-theorist; `sessions/archive/session-92/workshops/s92-adhoc-spectral-dimension-ds-flow-vs-cdt.md`; CONVERGED 3 rounds, 2026-05-24). landau-condensed-matter-theorist landed this corpus section as the final-turn (R3-B) EFFECTED-IN-SESSION deliverable. **Routing note** (same discipline as §18 / §19 / §20 / §22 / §23 precedents): the parent rules `.claude/rules/cross-pillar-bridge-anatomy.md §"Single-observable-per-triple structural filter"` AND `.claude/rules/phononic-framing.md §"IS Space, Not IN Space"` are orchestrator-only-edit (subagents EDIT-DENIED on `.claude/rules/` by harness convention — the recursion-attack-closure discipline of `methodology-wave-allowlist.md` + the AMRI discipline of `agent-standards.md`). This corpus section therefore carries BOTH the DIRECTIVE (the fair-comparison-observable discipline for spectral-dimension / diffusion-window comparisons) AND the per-instance K=1 calibration corpus; the **orchestrator mirrors the one-line directive** into `cross-pillar-bridge-anatomy.md §"Single-observable-per-triple structural filter"` (as a diffusion-window-observable specialization) AND `phononic-framing.md §"IS Space, Not IN Space"` (as a same-functional-different-scale fair-comparison sub-clause). The verbatim ORCHESTRATOR-RESERVED mirror text is given at the END of §24.0.

### §24.0 — DIRECTIVE (fair-comparison-observable discipline)

**The rule.** When comparing a substrate-IS spectral-dimension (or any return-probability / heat-kernel log-derivative observable `d_s(σ) = −2 d ln P(σ)/d ln σ`) against a laboratory-IN / external-framework reference (CDT, asymptotic-safety, causal-set), the comparison MUST pre-register the **(observable, diffusion-window) pair** on BOTH sides BEFORE any dimensional-reduction verdict. Specifically:

1. **Do NOT compare a σ→0 asymptotic to a windowed observable.** The σ→0 limit `lim_{σ→0} d_s(σ)` (the Weyl / Minakshisundaram–Pleijel exponent = manifold dimension) and the energy-resolved windowed value `d_s(σ_*)` at a finite diffusion scale `σ_* ~ 1/E_0²` are DIFFERENT functionals of the SAME return probability `P(σ)`. They can differ by an arbitrary amount on one and the same heat kernel (the textbook 1D van-Hove case: `d_s(E_0) → 1` at the fold while `d_s(σ→0) = d` bulk — both hold simultaneously). A verdict that proves one and asserts it about the other is an observable-conflation overclaim.

2. **Match the SCALE-TYPE, not the summand.** External-framework dimensional reductions (CDT's `d_s → 2`) are intermediate-window `P(σ)`-SHAPE statements, never σ→0 limits and never summand decompositions. The fair comparison applies the SAME universal functional `Φ: P(σ) ↦ −2 d ln P/d ln σ` to both return probabilities at the SAME KIND of scale (intermediate-window ↔ intermediate-window). The bridge map IS `Φ` itself — no summand-matching (`M⁴` vs fiber) is required, because the reduction is a shape statement at fixed scale-type.

3. **Pin the diffusion window to the physically relevant energy.** State the single fold-/feature-matched diffusion time `σ_* = 1/E_0²` (Gaussian weight `e^{−σλ²}` peaks at the feature energy `E_0`) and the scan band; the plateau metric is `min_{σ∈band} d_s(σ)` over that band, compared to the external framework's INTERMEDIATE-window value, NOT its IR value and NOT the manifold dimension.

4. **Separate the response-axis from the DOS-axis.** A measured `δd_s` is the response of `d_s` to whatever quantity was PERTURBED. If the perturbation is a gap / condensation order parameter at FIXED background DOS, the result bounds the gap response and is SILENT on the DOS-feature observable. The DOS-feature observable requires the normal-state (un-gapped) spectrum with the DOS feature INTACT.

5. **The discriminating sub-quantity is the directly-fitted energy-axis DOS exponent `γ_E`.** An impedance product `Z = ρ_E·v_g` (or any product constraint) is a CONSISTENCY CHECK, NOT a lock on `γ_E`: for a band edge `E−E_0 ~ |k|^n`, `ρ_E ~ |E−E_0|^{−(1−1/n)}` and `v_g ~ |E−E_0|^{+(1−1/n)}`, so `Z = ρ_E·v_g = const` for the ENTIRE family `γ_E = 1 − 1/n ∈ [1/2, 1)` (Sage-exact). Finite `Z` neither forbids nor forces any `γ_E`. Reading a PRODUCT constraint as a FACTOR constraint that fixes `γ_E = 1/2` is an algebra error. The mechanism verdict is the fitted `γ_E` directly (cumulative-count estimator `N(λ) − N(E_0) ~ sign(λ−E_0)·|λ−E_0|^{1−γ_E}` near `E_0`, robustness-checked across fit windows).

**Why this is a structural discipline, not a per-case convention.** Conflating the σ→0 asymptotic with the windowed observable is a `phononic-framing.md §"IS Space, Not IN Space"` violation at the observable layer: the substrate IS the return probability `P(σ)`; `d_s(σ→0)` and `d_s(σ_*)` are two intrinsic functionals of it, and a comparison that silently substitutes one for the other treats the laboratory-IN framework's scale-type as authoritative over the substrate's own. It is the spectral-dimension specialization of the `cross-pillar-bridge-anatomy.md §"Single-observable-per-triple structural filter"`: the (observable, diffusion-window) pair is the structural coordinate that must be fixed before a slot-split (here: "no reduction" vs "reduction") verdict is licensed.

**Status**: SUGGESTION at K=2 (K=1 = S92 AH-PF-1 scale-type axis; K=2 = S93 W7-3 observable-identity axis — see §24.2). Promotes to MANDATORY at K=3 distinct calibration instances per `feedback_rules-compensate-missing-structure.md`.

A verdict proving the asymptotic and asserting it about the windowed observable is an observable-conflation overclaim. Fair comparison applies the SAME functional `Φ: P(σ) ↦ −2 d ln P/d ln σ` at the SAME scale-type (intermediate-window ↔ intermediate-window); the bridge map IS `Φ`, no summand-matching required. The discriminating sub-quantity is the directly-fitted energy-axis DOS exponent `γ_E` (cumulative-count estimator); any impedance/product constraint `Z = ρ_E·v_g` is a CONSISTENCY CHECK, not a lock (`Z = const` for the whole family `γ_E = 1−1/n ∈ [1/2,1)`, Sage-exact). The substrate IS the return probability `P(σ) = Tr e^{−σ D_K²}`; `d_s(σ→0)` (Weyl asymptotic) and `d_s(σ_*)` (windowed at the feature energy) are two intrinsic functionals of it. Comparing the substrate's σ→0 asymptotic to an external framework's intermediate-window value is a container-thinking violation at the observable layer — it substitutes the lab framework's scale-type for the substrate's. ### §24.2 — K=2 calibration instance (S93 W7-3; observable-identity axis)

> **Provenance**: S93 W7-3 workshop (kaluza-klein-theorist × landau-condensed-matter-theorist; `sessions/archive/session-93/workshops/s93-w7-3-gamma-e-dos-exponent-estimator.md`; CONVERGED, 2026-05-25). Orchestrator-landed from the FLAGGED Effected-In-Session block.

The same-functional-fair-comparison directive applies not only at the scale-type layer (σ→0 Weyl asymptotic `d_s(σ→0)=dim SU(3)=8` vs windowed `d_s(σ_*)`; the AH-PF-1 K=1 instance) but at the OBSERVABLE-IDENTITY layer: a van-Hove criterion (`min d_s<3`) calibrated on the S52 graph-Laplacian functional `Φ_graph-Laplacian` (which dips to `d_s=2` in the intermediate window) was mis-carried to the heat-trace functional `Φ_heat-trace = −2 d ln Tr e^{−σD²}/d ln σ` (which cannot dip for a `62/3.20×10⁷ = 1.94×10⁻⁶`-mass δ-pile-up at ANY van-Hove order). The two are DISTINCT functionals (`Φ_graph-Laplacian ≠ Φ_heat-trace`), and a criterion calibrated on the first is not transportable to the second — the same structural error as the scale-type conflation, on an orthogonal axis (observable-identity vs scale-type). Corrective: retire `min d_s<3` as the van-Hove criterion; the van-Hove discriminator lives on the energy axis (`γ_E` / its band-dispersion source `v_g^{B2}(τ)`), where the `1.94×10⁻⁶` mass IS the entire signal. Provenance gate: S93-W7-3-FOLD-ENERGY-WINDOWED-DS-GATE (INDETERMINATE, structurally correct). **K-counter**: K=1 (AH-PF-1 scale-type, S92) → K=2 (W7-3 observable-identity, S93). Status: SUGGESTION (K<3).

#### §24.2 companion instance — S94 W6-19 (S93 W8-2 (0,0)-singlet area-functional conflation; observable-identity axis; NO K-counter advance)

The same-functional-fair-comparison discipline recurs at the OBSERVABLE-IDENTITY layer (the W7-3 axis) on a NEW substrate-IS/laboratory-IN pillar pair: the S93 W8-2 (0,0)-singlet "area-matching obstruction" (gate `S93-W8-2-NARROW-PATH-CASIMIR-TABLE`, INFO) conflated the **area-Casimir functional** `Φ_area : (p,q) ↦ √(C_2(p,q))` [→ 0 at (0,0), Sage-exact: `C_2(0,0)=0`] with the **lowest-eigenvalue functional** `Φ_floor : (p,q) ↦ min|λ|_{(p,q)}` [→ 0.819741 M_KK at (0,0); the fiber-embedding `H_F=C^{16}` ground mode on the trivial SU(3) sector]. The W8-2 INFO caveat read `Φ_floor(0,0) ≠ 0` as a failure of the `Φ_area → √(j(j+1))` LQG-area correspondence — a `Φ_area`-vs-`Φ_floor` observable-conflation. Fixing the SAME functional on both sides: `√(C_2(0,0)) = 0 = √(j(j+1))|_{j=0}` (Sage-exact, both vanish; the area-functionals AGREE EXACTLY at the trivial point), while the LQG area operator (Ashtekar-Lewandowski Eq. 5.4) sums over punctures and ANNIHILATES the j=0 no-puncture state (area gap Eq. 5.15 = smallest non-zero eigenvalue at j=1/2). The η_FB(0,0)=0.820 vs median 0.471 (rel-dev 0.741) outlier is the signature that `Φ_floor` does NOT track `Φ_area` at the trivial irrep. This is the EXACT analog of conflating `d_s(σ→0)` with `d_s(σ_*)` (the AH-PF-1 axis) and of conflating `Φ_graph-Laplacian` with `Φ_heat-trace` (the W7-3 axis), now on the LQG-area-operator ↔ SU(3)-area-Casimir pillar pair.

```
Φ_dispersion     := v_g^{B2}(τ) / n_dispersion(τ)   — the van Hove ORDER (energy-axis scaling exponent γ_E = 1 − 1/n)

Φ_DOS-singular   := ρ_singular = m·δ(E−E_0)         — the fixed multiplicity δ-WEIGHT (m=8; integrated weight ∫=8, NOT an order)
```

- **Mis-carriage mode A** (kk T1): reading `proven_1086` as a `Φ_dispersion` claim — FAIR against a dispersion claim, CATEGORY ERROR against a DOS claim. On `Φ_dispersion`, W7-22 (`S94-DS-GAMMA-E-RESOLUTION`, INFO, `audit_sha256=1b71fb67…`) found `|v_g(τ_fold)| = 0.0227` (ρ-pinned) / `0.0541` (band-ladder), BOTH ≥ 2.3× the `1e-2` floor at every τ-slice in `[0.15, 0.23]`; `n_dispersion = 1`; `first_gap` monotone-decreasing through the fold with no cusp/local-min/collapse. - **Mis-carriage mode B** (landau T2): promoting `Φ_DOS-singular`'s multiplicity (a WEIGHT) to a van Hove ORDER — a δ of any nonzero coefficient is equi-order (the `γ_E → 1` boundary); multiplicity ≠ order (kk T3 §2, conceded T4). **Resolution**: the ORDER lives on `Φ_dispersion` (= `n=1`, refuted as a singularity); the canonical PROVEN DOS is `Φ_DOS-continuum` (finite, velocity-slaved, = `14.02 = 1/(π·v_g)` at the W7-22 velocity to the digit); `Φ_DOS-singular` is a fixed δ-WEIGHT carrying no order. This is the EXACT analog of conflating `d_s(σ→0)` with `d_s(σ_*)` (the §24.1 AH-PF-1 scale-type axis) and of conflating `Φ_graph-Laplacian` with `Φ_heat-trace` (the §24.2 W7-3 observable-identity axis), now on the band-edge dispersion ↔ DOS functional pair.

## §27. FI-vs-RD Demarcation — Three-Axis Gated Predicate + Detector-vs-Dynamics Prior-Gate Disanalogy — DIRECTIVE annotation + K=1 calibration corpus (S96 W-2 lizzi×volovik)

> **Provenance**: S96 W-2 workshop "When Does a Functional-DEPENDENT Piece Falsify an FI-Survivor Claim?" (lizzi-spectral-functional-theorist × volovik-superfluid-universe-theorist; `sessions/archive/session-96/workshops/w2-fi-vs-rd-demarcation.md`; CONVERGED, 2026-05-30). Methodology ANNOTATION on the FI/RD-demarcation axis (the regulator-pin / cross-pillar corpus family — the FI-vs-RD / regulator-atlas content is anchored here, §25 / §10 / `regulator-pin-discipline.md §"four-axis orthogonality"`). This is a registry CORPUS entry; per `feedback_rules-directive-only-no-session-info.md` the per-instance demarcation content + the E3 K-counter advancement record live here, NOT in a curated `.claude/rules/*` file. Any rule-file pointer (e.g. a one-line cross-reference from `regulator-pin-discipline.md §"four-axis orthogonality"` or `math-scripts.md §"Multiplicative-normalization cancellation invariants"`) is a directive-only one-liner STAGED for the orchestrator; the substance is this corpus section. Sole-writer: volovik (R3-B corpus landing, S96 W-2 close).

### §27.1 — The DIRECTIVE: the three-axis gated demarcation predicate

An RD piece's effect on an FI-survivor claim is adjudicated by the GATED conjunction **DP-0 ∧ DP-1 ∧ DP-2, evaluated in the F-functor order (substrate → methodology → audit)** per `epistemic-discipline.md §"Layer-Decomposition"`. The order is FORCED by the F-functor direction, not stipulated.

- **DP-0 (admissibility — substrate-physics gate, evaluated FIRST).** Is the regulator R an admissible DETECTOR for THIS observable O? A regulator is admissible for a spectral-moment ratio iff it is a UV-only operation preserving O's IR-accumulation structure. 3-valued, substrate-DERIVED (not stipulated): {admissible / inadmissible-for-this-channel / admissible-but-self-inconsistent}. If INADMISSIBLE ⇒ O is OFF THE BOARD for R; DP-1/DP-2 are not asked. DP-0 closes the SYMMETRIC error pair — over-FI (privileging the FI family without a substrate reason) AND over-RD (crediting an inadmissible RD family); both are the same sin (asserting admissibility without a substrate check). The "admissible-but-self-inconsistent" tag is itself substrate-derived (the rising a-ratio carving the f-atlas: Gaussian-cutoff f₂/f₄=4.19 > the `1/0.6808 = 1.469` SDW-layer-series convergence bound ⇒ the Gaussian f's OWN series diverges, so it is an admissible heat-trace apparatus but self-inconsistent for the EFT-control verdict).
- **DP-1 (transversality — methodology-floor gate, INDEXED BY THE STATED VERDICT).** Given R admissible, does R's variation move O transverse (codim ≥ 1) or collinear to the *named* verdict's band boundary? Transverse ⇒ harmless-TO-THAT-VERDICT; collinear ⇒ falsifying-TO-THAT-VERDICT. The verdict index is MANDATORY: transversality is a property of (RD piece, verdict), NOT of (RD piece) alone. "Harmless-RD" is as ill-posed as unscoped "FI-survivor"; BOTH carry mandatory indices — FI-survivor carries (family-scope, verdict-axis); harmless-RD carries (verdict-index, admissibility-tag).
- **DP-2 (FI mechanism — audit-floor gate, tier-2 ONLY).** Given R admissible and within-family FI established, is the FI empirical (moments do NOT factor `w(L)·g_n`; STRONG, substrate-physics content) or structural-cancellation (factor identically; Phi-trivial tautology per `math-scripts.md §"Multiplicative-normalization cancellation invariants"`, K=3 MANDATORY)? `mult_cancellation=False` STRENGTHENS the within-family FI (empirical agreement that could have failed). Applies ONLY to tier-2 (f-CONTAINING) objects; tier-1 (f-FREE) objects SKIP it (no family to test).

**Dual to the predicate (survivor side): the three-stratum ladder.** tier-1 (f-FREE, UNQUALIFIED — the apparatus does not enter the object); tier-2 (f-INVARIANT-within-family, family-scope qualifier CLOSED by DP-0); axis-orthogonal q-flow (equilibrium-subtraction departure object — equilibrium floor EXACT/representative-independent + observed departure conditional-OPEN). Tier-1 alone is unqualified; the tier-2 qualifier is CLOSED; the orthogonal-axis qualifier is OPEN.

### §27.2 — The harmless-RD vs verdict-falsifying-RD discriminator (operational content of DP-1)

The verdict is a topological invariant of a curve (a sign, a zero-count, a band-position ratio); the RD piece is harmless iff it lives in a coordinate ORTHOGONAL to the invariant, falsifying iff COLLINEAR with it. Two distinct harmless-RD STABILITY CLASSES:

- **STABILIZER-membership (unconditional).** RD = pure positive multiplicative scale `S(R)` AND I(O) = sign/zero-structure ⇒ `sign(S(R)·I) = sign(I)` identically; harmless for ALL parameter values and ALL magnitudes, unconditionally. Calibration: WRONSKIAN-FI `W^R = K_R · 2(R_K')³`, K_R>0 a τ-flat positive scalar, 9–11 OOM drift (`K_zeta/K_SD=2.938e9`, `ratio_flat_zeta=7.30e-13`; S96-SDW-WRONSKIAN-FI `audit_sha256=3dd0235a…`).
- **MAGNITUDE-domination (conditional).** RD = sub-dominant additive term, harmless only WHILE `|S(R)| < |dominant|`; fails near a dominant-term zero-crossing. Calibration: SADDLE-REGINV loop slope sign-flips (zeta +18,329.74, f* +118,378.30, Gaussian −145.99; `loop_sign_flip_across_schemes=True`) but |−145.99| ≪ +59,252 tree ⇒ no interior τ* solves `dΓ_1loop/dτ = −dS_tree/dτ`; conditional-harmless, fails in the censored τ>τ_NEC region (S96-SDW-SADDLE-REGINV `audit_sha256=1e32d548…`; FULLDOMAIN `c25a6909…`). The SAME Gaussian loop is transverse-to-the-saddle-count but COLLINEAR-to-any-loop-keyed-verdict (a one-loop running, a loop-induced curvature) — proving DP-1's verdict-index is mandatory.

**Verdict-falsifying-RD**: the RD piece modulates the SAME ratio the band boundary tests. Calibration: EFT-CONTROL f-coefficient ratio crosses r=1.0 (Gaussian r₀=1.807761 FAIL / Mellin r₀=0.014375 PASS, 126× spread on the fixed D_K spectrum; S96-SDW-EFT-CONTROL canonical `74158937…`). NOTE: the CC-GAP across-PV shift (36.86%; S96-SDW-CC-GAP `da899b4d…`) is NOT verdict-falsifying — it is DP-0-INADMISSIBLE (off-board), the correct verdict over a naive "COLLINEAR-conditionally-falsifying" mis-file.

### §27.3 — K=1 calibration instance: the detector-vs-dynamics prior-gate disanalogy (E3, certified-broken)

The genuinely-new methodology fact the workshop produced, recorded as a CONTROLLED analogy with a NAMED disanalogy (E3-CERTIFY-WITH-CAVEAT) — NOT a clean unification:

- **HOLDS (the axis-parametrized prior-gate STRUCTURE is real):** every framework axis carries a logically-prior admissibility gate. Regulator atlas → DP-0 (is R an admissible detector for moment-ratio O?). q-flow → C10 (is ρ_vac~H^n with n=2 the admissible departure law for the equilibrium-subtracted vacuum?). The demarcation analysis is NOT regulator-atlas-only in this weak structural sense; the §27.1 three-stratum ladder is INDEPENDENT of whether the gates unify.
- **BREAKS (the two gates are NOT the same KIND of admissibility):** DP-0 (regulator atlas) is a **DETECTOR-consistency filter** — DERIVABLE and CLOSED, because the substrate's spectral structure already fixes which probes are self-consistent (the UV-only⇒IR-preserving criterion follows from K_CSUB_R, S95 W1-2 `84c5ec48…`, corpus §25.2: a finite-N PV subtracts POLYNOMIAL moments and cannot regulate the small-λ IR-accumulation tower — no new physics needed to discharge it). C10 (q-flow) is a **DYNAMICAL-LAW ansatz** — ASSUMED and OPEN, because the substrate's actual out-of-equilibrium departure trajectory is input the structure does not (yet) fix (n=2 is the simple-fluid Gibbs-Duhem ansatz, Atlas-04 `ASSUMED-PARTIALLY-PROVEN`; no extant computation derives it).

**The discriminator that decides the disanalogy: DISCHARGEABILITY.** A detector-consistency filter is CLOSED *because* it is a structural fact the substrate's spectral structure determines; a dynamical-law ansatz is OPEN *because* it is a hypothesis about how the substrate evolves. The discharge axis and the detector-vs-dynamics axis are the SAME cut viewed from two sides. Calling C10 "the q-flow DP-0" without the disanalogy LAUNDERS an open dynamical assumption (C10) into the closed-detector-filter bucket (DP-0) — the over-reach the FI/RD demarcation analysis exists to catch (mirror of the over-FI error: privileging a unification without a substrate reason). The substrate-first reading: the regulator atlas's prior gate is a consistency filter the substrate's spectral structure fixes (closed); the q-flow axis's prior gate is a dynamical hypothesis about substrate evolution (open) — and the framework's CC closure rests on the OPEN one. Naming that asymmetry is the load-bearing content; it is the q-flow side's exposed CC surface.

**Corpus tag**: two-axis-prior-gate structure CONFIRMED; one-DP-0-across-two-axes BROKEN; named disanalogy = detector-admissibility(closed-detector-filter, derivable) vs dynamical-law-admissibility(open-dynamical-ansatz, assumed). Discharge path for the OPEN gate (CF-S97-W2-2, q-flow axis): derive n in ρ_vac~H^n from the q-theory EoS `P=−ε+μq` + the substrate out-of-equilibrium q-trajectory (the q-flow analog of how K_CSUB_R derived PV-inadmissibility on the regulator atlas).

### §28.1 — The DIRECTIVE: the Non-LI-Deformation Necessity precondition

**Named precondition (Non-LI-Deformation Necessity for intrinsically-rigid matter-sector asymmetries).** On the orbital spectral triple `(A_K, H_K, D_K, J)` on Jensen-deformed SU(3), `A_K = ℂ ⊕ ℍ ⊕ M₃(ℂ)`, `H_K = ⊕_{(p,q)} V_{(p,q)} ⊗ ℂ^{m(p,q)}`, with Peter-Weyl action `π(a) = ⊕ π_{(p,q)}(a) ⊗ 1_{m(p,q)}`: let `O` be a matter-sector observable that is an asymmetry or inter-class hierarchy across the J-orbit / multiplicity structure (e.g. `O = η_B`; or `O = R_cross = m_{gen,i}/m_{gen,j}`). Then the GATED conjunction **W1 ∧ W2 ∧ W3** forbids `O`'s intrinsic value:

- **W1 (reality wall)** — `[J, D_K + δ] = 0` for the deformation `δ`. SATISFIABLE; reality is INNOCENT (it constrains `δ`'s form block-by-block but does not forbid it). The obstruction is NOT reality.
- **W2 (homogeneity wall)** — left-invariance ⇒ the representation `π` is multiplicity-scalar (`D_K` left-invariant ⇒ commutes with the right-translation generators ⇒ `π` acts on the left-regular factor only ⇒ scalar on `ℂ^{m(p,q)}`); equivalently block-diagonality / `p_1[SU(3)] = 0` / cross-sector `Y = 0`.
- **W3 (inner-fluctuation impotence, consequence)** — every `A_K`-built form (inner `Σ aᵢ[D_K,bᵢ]`, twisted-inner `Ω¹_σ` for any `σ ∈ Aut(A_K)`, opposite-action `JAJ⁻¹`) is multiplicity-scalar (Skolem–Noether: `Aut(A_K)` is multiplicity-blind), hence respects BOTH walls, hence breaks NEITHER. `O` is INVARIANT under the entire `A_K`-built deformation orbit.

**Design-rule corollary**: any mechanism discharging `O` to its observed nonzero value MUST be an EXTERNAL non-left-invariant fibre connection `δ = ε_X` that breaks W2 while PRESERVING W1, non-gauge-removable (`P_nLI = ‖ε_X‖² > 0`). The reality axiom is NEVER sacrificed; only homogeneity is broken. The S98 planner builds any such gate as a non-LI mixing/deformation gate by construction, using the closed baryogenesis gate as the structural template (same two walls, same external-datum escape, same `P_nLI > 0` non-removability check).

## §29. IS-not-IN A/B/C Category Distinction — a competing-mechanism intermediate is neither a substrate pin nor an observational datum — DIRECTIVE annotation + K=1 calibration corpus (S116 W-3 efold-binding-vs-container volovik × mack)

**Companion rule**: `phononic-framing.md §"IS Space, Not IN Space — Mandatory Reframe"` (the bare cross-reference pointer lives in the rule body; this is the per-instance calibration home, mirroring the §23 / §24 pattern where phononic-framing.md directives carry their corpora here).

### DIRECTIVE

Any number bearing on a framework observable belongs to exactly ONE of three categories, and the governing discipline differs by category:

| Category | What it is | Governing rule | Transfers to this framework? |
|:--|:--|:--|:--|
| **(A) substrate pin** | a value a substrate COMPUTATION consumes (a constant standing in for a calculation) | `substrate-first-canonical-sourcing.md §(i)` (provenance must be substrate-first, not external-paper-canonical) | N/A — it IS the framework's own |
| **(B) observational datum** | the external number the framework is TESTED against (`Ω_k`, `n_s`, the k-window, `H_0`, …) | falsification (`epistemic-discipline.md`) — supposed to be external; "facing the data" requires it | binds as a TEST target, regardless of mechanism |
| **(C) competing-mechanism internal intermediate** | a number internal to a DIFFERENT framework's machinery (a bookkeeping quantity of a rival mechanism) | none — it is neither sourced nor tested-against | **NO — it does not cross the bridge** |

**Mis-filing test (both directions fail).** Mis-filing a (C) number as **(A)** [invoking `substrate-first-canonical-sourcing.md §(i)` to forbid it as a "container import"] makes every observational bound reclassifiable-as-forbidden ⇒ unfalsifiable-by-relabeling (the failure mode `epistemic-discipline.md` exists to block). Mis-filing a (C) number as **(B)** [treating it as a binding observational requirement] imports a rival mechanism's bookkeeping as if it were data, and lays that rival's ruler against a substrate-IS quantity (a category mismatch read as a substrate deficit). The discriminator question: *does any instrument MEASURE this number?* If yes → (B). If it is consumed by a substrate computation → (A). If it is internal to a competing mechanism's machinery and no instrument measures it → (C), and it does NOT transfer.

### K=1 calibration instance — the inflation e-fold count `N_e ≥ 3.1`

`N_E_THRESHOLD = 3.1` (S116-W6 Def 6, "external horizon/flatness target") is **category (C)**: the e-fold count INFLATION's dilution mechanism needs to discharge horizon+flatness on the FRW container. NO instrument measures `N_e ≥ 3.1` (Planck measures `Ω_k = 0.0007 ± 0.0019`, `n_s = 0.9649 ± 0.0042`, the k-window). The workshop's initial confusion was treating `3.1` as one of two things when it was a third: volovik's R1 mis-filed it as (A) (the `§(i)` citation, withdrawn R2); mack's Re:V2 mis-filed it as (B) (the "external datum tested against" reading, corrected). Filed correctly as (C), the apparent disagreement dissolves: the (B) obligations (`Ω_k`, `n_s`, k-window) BIND and are KEPT; the (C) number `3.1` is RETIRED as non-transferring. The substrate discharges the (B) obligations `3.1` proxied by three native mechanisms — horizon → acoustic white-hole sealing (PROVEN S85); flatness → a_2 acoustic-form ∧ uniform-conformal-factor ⇒ `Ω_k = 0` (OPEN, `CF-S117-A2-OMEGAK-ACOUSTIC-FORM`); scale-range → GGE `|β(k)|²` bandwidth + window-wide tilt (OPEN/CRITICAL, `CF-S117-TRANSIT-PS-67-WINDOW-WIDE`) — NONE counting e-folds. `N_e` is a Layer-3 translation diagnostic (the log of the emergent FRW scale factor read off afterward), never a substrate-binding gate. Falsifier-surface landing: `falsifier-master-inventory.md` Row #93.
